=== www/js/main.js ===
// Entry point: boot, bottom navigation, Android back button, router wiring.
import * as store from './store.js';
import { setRouter, handleBack } from './router.js';
import { applyTheme, renderHome, renderRank, renderShop, renderMore } from './screens.js';
import { startNiko, stopNiko } from './play.js';
import { startSeq, stopSeq } from './playseq.js';
import { sfx, unlock } from './sound.js';
import { $, $$, sheetOpen, closeSheet } from './ui.js';

const TABS = { home: renderHome, rank: renderRank, shop: renderShop, more: renderMore };
let tab = 'home';

// unread messages and notifications show as a badge on More
function refreshBadge() {
  const b = $('#moreBadge');
  if (!b) return;
  const n = store.unread();
  b.textContent = n > 9 ? '9+' : String(n);
  b.hidden = !n;
}

function go(t = tab) {
  stopNiko();
  stopSeq();
  closeSheet(true);
  document.body.classList.remove('playing');
  tab = TABS[t] ? t : 'home';
  $$('#nav button').forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.tab === tab));
  const root = $('#screen');
  root.innerHTML = '';
  TABS[tab](root);
  refreshBadge();
}

function start(game, opts) {
  closeSheet(true);
  if (game === 'ss') startSeq(opts);
  else startNiko(opts);
}

setRouter({ go, start });

function boot() {
  store.load();
  applyTheme();
  $$('#nav button').forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { go(b.dataset.tab); }));
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', unlock, { once: true, capture: true });
  store.touchDay();
  document.addEventListener('nk:inbox', refreshBadge);
  go('home');

  // Android hardware back (Capacitor). In a plain browser this is a no-op.
  try {
    const App = window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.Plugins && window.Capacitor.Plugins.App;
    if (App && App.addListener) {
      App.addListener('backButton', () => {
        if (sheetOpen()) return closeSheet();
        if (handleBack()) return;
        if (tab !== 'home') return go('home');
        App.exitApp && App.exitApp();
      });
      // sound can't play in the background
      App.addListener('appStateChange', (st) => { if (!st.isActive) document.dispatchEvent(new Event('nk:background')); });
    }
  } catch (e) { /* not native */ }

  // Offline for the web version; the native app already ships its files.
  const native = window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.isNativePlatform && window.Capacitor.isNativePlatform();
  if (!native && 'serviceWorker' in navigator && location.protocol.startsWith('http')) navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(() => {});
}

boot();

=== www/js/online.js ===
// Play modes: computer, online match, private room.
// Real multiplayer needs a game server, which this build does not have yet. The flows below are complete on screen,
// and every network call goes through `net` so a real server can replace it later without touching the screens.
import * as store from './store.js';
import { BOTS } from './bots.js';
import { $, $$, sheet, closeSheet, toast } from './ui.js';
import { startGame } from './router.js';

export const net = {
  live: false,
  async findMatch() { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2600)); return { ok: false, reason: 'offline' }; },
  newRoomCode() { const a = 'ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789'; return Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => a[(Math.random() * a.length) | 0]).join(''); },
  async joinRoom() { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 900)); return { ok: false, reason: 'offline' }; },
};

const I = {
  cpu: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="M9 2h2v3h2V2h2v3h2.500A1.500 1.500 0 0 1 20 6.500V9h3v2h-3v2h3v2h-3v2.500a1.500 1.500 0 0 1-1.500 1.500H16v3h-2v-3h-2v3h-2v-3H7.500A1.500 1.500 0 0 1 6 17.500V15H3v-2h3v-2H3V9h3V6.500A1.500 1.500 0 0 1 7.500 5H9zM8 8v8h8V8z"/></svg>',
  globe: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9.500"/><path d="M2.500 12h19M12 2.500c3 3 4 6 4 9.500s-1 6.500-4 9.500c-3-3-4-6-4-9.500s1-6.500 4-9.500z"/></svg>',
  room: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="M4 3h11a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v1h3a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v14h2v2H2v-2h2zm2 2v14h8V5zm5 6h2v2h-2z"/></svg>',
};
const NAMES = { nk: 'Niko Kadi', ss: 'Special Sequence' };

const opt = (id, ic, t, sub) => `<button class="opt" id="${id}"><span class="ic">${I[ic]}</span><span>${t}<small>${sub}</small></span></button>`;

export function showModes(game, { computer }) {
  const d = store.get();
  const root = (html, bind) => { const sh = sheet(html); bind(sh.el); };
  const modes = () => root(`<h2>${NAMES[game]}</h2><p>How do you want to play?</p>
    ${opt('mC', 'cpu', 'Play the computer', 'Four levels, Easy to Expert')}
    ${opt('mO', 'globe', 'Online match', 'Quick game against players worldwide')}
    ${opt('mR', 'room', 'Private room', 'Make a room and invite friends with a code')}`, (el) => {
    $('#mC', el).onclick = () => { closeSheet(true); computer(game); };
    $('#mO', el).onclick = online;
    $('#mR', el).onclick = rooms;
  });

  const online = () => root(`<h2>Online match</h2><p>You'll be matched with players of a similar rank.</p>
    <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="back">Back</button><button class="btn" id="find">Find a match</button></div>`, (el) => {
    $('#back', el).onclick = modes;
    $('#find', el).onclick = async () => {
      el.innerHTML = `<h2>Looking for players</h2><div class="searching"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div><p style="text-align:center">Searching for a ${NAMES[game]} table…</p><div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="cx">Cancel</button></div>`;
      let off = false;
      $('#cx', el).onclick = () => { off = true; modes(); };
      const r = await net.findMatch(game);
      if (off) return;
      if (!r.ok) root(`<h2>No connection to the game servers</h2><p>Online matches are not available in this version yet, so nobody could be found. Your rank is untouched.</p>
        <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="b">Back</button><button class="btn" id="cpu">Play the computer</button></div>`, (e2) => {
        $('#b', e2).onclick = modes; $('#cpu', e2).onclick = () => { closeSheet(true); computer(game); };
      });
    };
  });

  const rooms = () => root(`<h2>Private room</h2><p>Play with friends. Everyone who has the code can take a seat.</p>
    ${opt('rC', 'room', 'Create a room', 'You get a code to share')}
    ${opt('rJ', 'globe', 'Join with a code', 'Enter the code a friend sent you')}
    <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="back">Back</button></div>`, (el) => {
    $('#back', el).onclick = modes;
    $('#rC', el).onclick = create;
    $('#rJ', el).onclick = join;
  });

  const create = () => {
    const code = net.newRoomCode();
    let n = d.count[game] || 2;
    const draw = (el) => {
      el.innerHTML = `<h2>Your room</h2><p>Share this code so friends can join.</p>
        <div class="roomcode" id="code">${code}</div>
        <div class="row"><button class="btn small grey" id="copy">Copy code</button><button class="btn small grey" id="share">Share</button></div>
        <h3>Seats</h3><div class="seg" id="cnt">${[2, 3, 4].map((x) => `<button data-n="${x}" class="${x === n ? 'on' : ''}">${x} players</button>`).join('')}</div>
        <div class="seats">${Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => `<div class="seat-row${i ? ' empty' : ''}"><b>${i ? 'Waiting for a friend…' : 'You (host)'}</b></div>`).join('')}</div>
        <p class="note-sm">${net.live ? '' : 'Friends cannot join rooms in this version yet. You can fill the empty seats with computer players and start now.'}</p>
        <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="back">Back</button><button class="btn" id="go">${'Start game'}</button></div>`;
      $$('#cnt button', el).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { n = +b.dataset.n; draw(el); }));
      $('#back', el).onclick = rooms;
      $('#copy', el).onclick = async () => { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code); toast('Code copied'); } catch (e) { toast('Code: ' + code); } };
      $('#share', el).onclick = async () => { try { if (navigator.share) await navigator.share({ title: 'Niko Kadi', text: `Join my Niko Kadi room with code ${code}` }); else { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code); toast('Code copied'); } } catch (e) { /* cancelled */ } };
      $('#go', el).onclick = () => {
        const li = BOTS[d.level[game]] ? d.level[game] : 2;
        d.count[game] = n; d.game = game; store.save();
        closeSheet(true);
        startGame(game, { botId: BOTS[li].id, count: n, rules: d.rules });
      };
    };
    const sh = sheet(''); draw(sh.el);
  };

  const join = () => root(`<h2>Join a room</h2><p>Type the 5 letter code.</p>
    <input type="text" id="jc" maxlength="5" placeholder="CODE" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" style="text-align:center;letter-spacing:.3em;text-transform:uppercase">
    <p id="jerr" class="note-sm"></p>
    <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="back">Back</button><button class="btn" id="jg">Join</button></div>`, (el) => {
    $('#back', el).onclick = rooms;
    $('#jg', el).onclick = async () => {
      const c = $('#jc', el).value.trim().toUpperCase();
      if (c.length !== 5) { $('#jerr', el).textContent = 'The code has 5 letters or numbers.'; return; }
      $('#jg', el).disabled = true;
      const r = await net.joinRoom(c);
      $('#jg', el).disabled = false;
      if (!r.ok) $('#jerr', el).textContent = `Could not reach room ${c}. Online rooms are not available in this version yet.`;
    };
  });

  modes();
}

=== www/js/play.js ===
// Niko Kadi table: rendering, input, bots, timer, sound and results.
import * as E from './engine.js';
import { chooseAction, applyAction, pickOpponents } from './bots.js';
import { cardEl, backEl, wait, SUIT_TXT, rankLabel } from './cards.js';
import { playerAvatar, avatarHTML } from './avatars.js';
import { ICON } from './icons.js';
import { sfx } from './sound.js';
import * as store from './store.js';
import { rankInfo } from './rp.js';
import { $, $$, esc, toast, sheet, closeSheet } from './ui.js';
import { go, pushBack } from './router.js';
import { SLOTS, fly, flyBack, rl, suitHTML, plateHTML, fanHTML, layoutHand, menuSheet, dragify, HELP_NK } from './table.js';

let G = null;

export function stopNiko() {
  if (!G) return;
  G.stopped = true;
  clearInterval(G.tmr);
  clearTimeout(G.botT);
  G.unback && G.unback();
  G = null;
}

export function startNiko({ botId, count, rules }) {
  stopNiko();
  const d = store.get();
  const opp = pickOpponents(botId, count - 1);
  const list = [{ name: d.name, avatar: playerAvatar(d), human: true }, ...opp.map((b) => ({ name: b.name, bot: b, avatar: b.avatar }))];
  const players = list.map((p) => ({ p, k: Math.random() })).sort((a, b) => a.k - b.k).map((x) => x.p); // random seats: you don't always start
  const s = E.newGame({ players, rules });
  G = {
    s, me: players.findIndex((p) => p.human), botId, count, rules,
    sel: [], busy: true, paused: false, stopped: false, turnKey: -1,
    deadline: 0, lastSec: 99, tmr: 0, botT: 0, msg: '', els: new Map(), rivals: opp,
  };
  store.get().lastBot.nk = botId;
  document.body.classList.add('playing');
  G.unback = pushBack(() => openMenu());
  build();
  renderAll(true);
  sfx('shuffle');
  setTimeout(() => sfx('deal'), 350);
  wait(900).then(() => { if (!G || G.stopped) return; G.busy = false; nextStep(); });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ skeleton
function build() {
  $('#screen').innerHTML = `<div class="table" id="tbl">
    <button class="hint" id="snd" aria-label="Sound"></button>
    <button class="menu" id="menu" aria-label="Menu"><span class="ic">${ICON.list}</span></button>
    <div id="seats"></div>
    <div class="center"><div class="pile" id="drawP"></div><div class="pile" id="discP"></div></div>
    <div class="effect" id="eff" hidden></div>
    <div class="status" id="status"></div>
    <div class="mine" id="mine"></div>
    <div class="bar" id="bar"></div>
    <div class="hand" id="hand"></div>
  </div>`;
  dragify($('#drawP'), {
    can: () => canAct() && !!G.s.draw.length && E.summary(G.s).canTake,
    ghost: () => backEl(),
    rectOf: () => { const c = $('#drawP .card:last-of-type'); return c ? c.getBoundingClientRect() : null; },
    hit: (x, y) => y > $('#hand').getBoundingClientRect().top - 50,
    hi: () => $('#hand'),
    target: () => $('#hand').getBoundingClientRect(),
    onDrop: () => onAct('take', { noFly: true }),
    onTap: () => { if (canAct()) toast(E.summary(G.s).canTake ? 'Drag the pile to your hand, or press Take' : "You can't draw right now"); },
  });
  $('#menu').onclick = () => { sfx('click'); openMenu(); };
  $('#snd').onclick = () => { const d = store.get(); d.settings.sound = !d.settings.sound; store.save(); import('./sound.js').then((m) => m.configure({ enabled: d.settings.sound })); renderSnd(); if (d.settings.sound) sfx('click'); };
  renderSnd();
}
const renderSnd = () => { const b = $('#snd'); if (b) b.innerHTML = `<span class="ic">${store.get().settings.sound ? ICON.sound : ICON.mute}</span>`; };

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ rendering
const nm = (i) => (i === G.me ? 'You' : G.s.players[i].name);
const seatRect = (i) => { const el = $(`.plate[data-p="${i}"]`); return el ? el.getBoundingClientRect() : null; };

function renderAll(initial = false) {
  renderSeats();
  renderCenter();
  renderEffect();
  renderStatus();
  renderMine();
  renderHand(initial);
  renderBar();
}

function renderSeats() {
  const s = G.s, n = s.n, slots = SLOTS[n];
  let html = '';
  for (let k = 1; k < n; k++) {
    const i = (G.me + k) % n, p = s.players[i];
    const tag = p.declared != null && !p.out ? '<span class="tag">NIKO KADI</span>' : '';
    html += `<div class="seat ${slots[k - 1]}">${plateHTML(p, i, { active: s.cur === i && !s.over, tag })}${p.out ? '' : fanHTML(p.hand.length)}</div>`;
  }
  $('#seats').innerHTML = html;
}

function renderMine() {
  const s = G.s, p = s.players[G.me];
  const tag = p.declared != null && !p.out ? '<span class="tag">NIKO KADI</span>' : '';
  $('#mine').innerHTML = plateHTML(p, G.me, { active: s.cur === G.me && !s.over, tag });
}

function renderCenter() {
  const s = G.s;
  const dp = $('#drawP');
  dp.className = 'pile' + (s.draw.length ? '' : ' empty');
  let h = '';
  if (s.draw.length) {
    const nb = Math.min(3, s.draw.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < nb; i++) { const b = backEl(); b.style.transform = `translate(${(i - nb + 1) * 2}px,${(i - nb + 1) * 2}px)`; h += b.outerHTML; }
  }
  dp.innerHTML = h + `<span class="cnt">${s.draw.length}</span>`;
  const dc = $('#discP');
  dc.className = 'pile';
  dc.innerHTML = '';
  const top = s.discard.slice(-3);
  top.forEach((id, i) => {
    const el = cardEl(id);
    const depth = top.length - 1 - i;
    const tilt = depth === 0 ? 0 : (((id * 37) % 11) - 5) * (depth === 1 ? 1.1 : 1.6);
    el.style.transform = `rotate(${tilt}deg) translate(${depth * -3}px, ${depth * -2}px)`;
    dc.appendChild(el);
  });
}

function infoText() {
  const s = G.s, inf = E.info(s);
  const suits = [0, 1, 2, 3].filter((x) => inf.mask & (1 << x)).map(suitHTML).join('');
  return inf.rc != null ? `${suits} or ${rl(inf.rc === 12 ? 12 : inf.rc)}${inf.rc === 12 ? '/8' : ''}` : suits;
}

function renderEffect() {
  const s = G.s, el = $('#eff');
  let cls = '', html = '';
  if (s.over) { el.hidden = true; return; }
  if (s.pending) { cls = 'pen'; html = `<span class="big">${s.pending.count}</span> to draw`; }
  else if (s.turn.mustAnswer) { cls = 'q'; html = `Answer <span class="big">?</span>`; }
  else if (s.threat > 0) { cls = 'jmp'; html = `Skip <span class="big">×${s.threat}</span>`; }
  else if (s.demand) {
    cls = 'dem';
    html = s.demand.W >= 2 ? `Request <span class="big">${rl(s.demand.rank)}${suitHTML(s.demand.suit)}</span>` : `Suit <span class="big">${suitHTML(s.demand.suit)}</span>`;
  } else if (s.eff && s.eff.mask) {
    cls = 'q';
    html = `Follow <span class="big">${[0, 1, 2, 3].filter((x) => s.eff.mask & (1 << x)).map(suitHTML).join('')}</span>`;
  }
  if (!html) { el.hidden = true; el.dataset.k = ''; return; }
  const key = cls + html;
  el.className = 'effect ' + cls;
  el.innerHTML = html;
  if (el.dataset.k !== key) { el.hidden = true; void el.offsetWidth; } // restart the pop animation
  el.hidden = false;
  el.dataset.k = key;
}

function hintText() {
  const s = G.s, c = E.ctx(s);
  switch (c) {
    case 'answer': return `Answer the question with a normal card (${infoText()})`;
    case 'pending': return `Draw ${s.pending.count}, pass it on, or play an Ace`;
    case 'threat': return 'Counter with a Jack, or accept the skip';
    case 'demand': return s.demand.W >= 2 ? `Play the ${rl(s.demand.rank)}${suitHTML(s.demand.suit)} or an Ace` : `Play ${suitHTML(s.demand.suit)} or an Ace`;
    default: return s.turn.placed ? 'Play more, or pass' : `Your turn · match ${infoText()}`;
  }
}

function renderStatus() {
  const s = G.s, el = $('#status');
  if (s.over) { el.hidden = true; return; }
  el.hidden = false;
  if (s.cur === G.me) { el.className = 'status you'; el.innerHTML = hintText() + (!G.sel.length && !E.playableIds(s).size && E.summary(s).canTake ? '<br>No card fits. Tap Take when you are ready.' : ''); }
  else { el.className = 'status'; el.innerHTML = G.msg ? esc(G.msg) : `${esc(s.players[s.cur].name)} is thinking…`; }
}

const sortHand = (h) => h.slice().sort((a, b) => (E.isJoker(a) - E.isJoker(b)) || E.suitOf(a) - E.suitOf(b) || E.orderOf(a) - E.orderOf(b));

function renderHand(initial) {
  const s = G.s, p = s.players[G.me], box = $('#hand');
  const mine = s.cur === G.me && !s.over;
  const ok = mine ? E.playableIds(s) : new Set();
  const ids = sortHand(p.hand);
  for (const [id, el] of G.els) if (!ids.includes(id)) { el.remove(); G.els.delete(id); }
  const els = ids.map((id) => {
    let el = G.els.get(id);
    if (!el) {
      el = cardEl(id);
      dragify(el, {
        can: canAct,
        ghost: () => { const g = el.cloneNode(true); g.classList.remove('sel', 'dim', 'ok', 'dragging'); return g; },
        hit: (x, y) => y < $('#hand').getBoundingClientRect().top - 12,
        hi: () => $('.center'),
        target: () => $('#discP').getBoundingClientRect(),
        onDrop: () => dragPlay(id),
        onTap: () => onCard(id),
      });
      G.els.set(id, el);
      el.dataset.fresh = '1';
    }
    el.classList.toggle('sel', G.sel.includes(id));
    el.classList.toggle('dim', mine && !ok.has(id) && !G.sel.includes(id));
    el.classList.toggle('ok', mine && ok.has(id));
    box.appendChild(el);
    return el;
  });
  layoutHand(box, els);
  const drawR = $('#drawP').getBoundingClientRect();
  els.forEach((el, i) => {
    if (el.dataset.fresh) { delete el.dataset.fresh; if (!G.noFly) fly(el, drawR, { delay: initial ? 120 + i * 110 : (G.freshDelay || 0) + i * 0 }); }
  });
}

function renderBar() {
  const s = G.s, bar = $('#bar');
  if (s.over || s.cur !== G.me || G.busy || G.paused) { bar.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  const sm = E.summary(s), p = s.players[G.me];
  let h = '';
  if (sm.declared && E.canFall(p.hand)) h += `<button class="act nk done" disabled>${ICON.check} Called</button>`;
  else h += `<button class="act nk${sm.canDeclare ? '' : ' off'}" data-a="declare">Niko Kadi</button>`;
  if (sm.canAccept) h += `<button class="act" data-a="accept">Accept<small>SKIP</small></button>`;
  if (sm.canPass) h += `<button class="act" data-a="pass">Pass</button>`;
  if (sm.canTake) {
    const lose = p.hand.length + sm.takeCount > E.HAND_LIMIT;
    h += `<button class="act take${lose ? ' warn' : ' hot'}" data-a="take">Take<small>${sm.takeCount > 1 ? sm.takeCount + ' CARDS' : '1 CARD'}</small></button>`;
  }
  if (G.sel.length) h += `<button class="act go" data-a="play">Play</button>`;
  bar.innerHTML = h;
  $$('.act', bar).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => onAct(b.dataset.a)));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ events -> sound / motion
function capture() {
  const hand = new Map();
  for (const [id, el] of G.els) hand.set(id, el.getBoundingClientRect());
  return { hand, draw: $('#drawP').getBoundingClientRect() };
}

function describe(e) {
  const s = G.s;
  switch (e.t) {
    case 'play': return `${nm(e.p)} played ${e.cards.map(E.cardName).join(' ')}`;
    case 'draw': return e.n ? `${nm(e.p)} drew ${e.n}` : null;
    case 'demand': return e.demand.W >= 2 ? `${nm(e.p)} asks for ${rl(e.demand.rank)}${SUIT_TXT[e.demand.suit]}` : `${nm(e.p)} calls ${SUIT_TXT[e.demand.suit]}`;
    case 'cancel': return `${nm(e.p)} cancelled the ${e.what}`;
    case 'skip': return `${nm(e.p)} skipped`;
    case 'reverse': return 'Direction reversed';
    case 'declare': return `${nm(e.p)}: Niko Kadi!`;
    case 'star': return `${nm(e.p)} ran out of time`;
    case 'out': return `${nm(e.p)} is out`;
    case 'forward': return `${nm(e.p)} passes it on`;
    case 'counter': return `${nm(e.p)} counters with a Jack`;
    case 'refill': return 'Shuffling the pile';
  }
  return null;
}

// state is already final when this runs; this plays the sound and flies the cards, then waits.
async function present(ev, pre) {
  renderAll();
  let t = 0;
  const at = (ms, fn) => setTimeout(() => { if (G && !G.stopped) fn(); }, ms);
  const msgs = [];
  for (const e of ev) {
    const m = describe(e);
    if (m) msgs.push(m);
    switch (e.t) {
      case 'play': {
        at(t, () => sfx('place'));
        if (e.kind === 'A') at(t + 100, () => sfx('ace'));
        e.cards.forEach((c, i) => {
          const node = $(`#discP .card[data-id="${c}"]`);
          const from = e.p === G.me ? pre.hand.get(c) : seatRect(e.p);
          if (node && from && !(G.noFly && e.p === G.me)) fly(node, from, { delay: t + i * 70 });
        });
        t += 240 + 70 * (e.cards.length - 1);
        break;
      }
      case 'draw': {
        if (!e.n) break;
        const to = seatRect(e.p);
        e.cards.forEach((c, i) => {
          at(t + i * 90, () => sfx('draw'));
          if (e.p === G.me) { const el = G.els.get(c); if (el) { /* already animated by renderHand */ } }
          else flyBack(pre.draw, to, { delay: t + i * 90 });
        });
        t += 160 + Math.min(e.n, 5) * 90;
        break;
      }
      case 'penalty': at(t, () => sfx('penalty')); break;
      case 'skip': at(t, () => sfx('skip')); t += 260; break;
      case 'reverse': at(t, () => sfx('reverse')); t += 200; break;
      case 'declare': at(t, () => sfx('niko')); t += 350; break;
      case 'cancel': case 'forward': at(t, () => sfx('ace')); break;
      case 'refill': at(t, () => sfx('shuffle')); t += 350; break;
      case 'star': at(t, () => sfx('star')); t += 300; break;
      case 'out': at(t, () => sfx('out')); t += 300; break;
      case 'turn': if (e.p === G.me) at(t + 60, () => sfx('turn')); break;
      case 'win': at(t + 150, () => sfx(e.p === G.me ? 'win' : 'lose')); t += 450; break;
    }
  }
  if (msgs.length) G.msg = msgs.slice(-2).join(' · ');
  const st = $('#status');
  if (st && G.s.cur !== G.me && !G.s.over) st.textContent = G.msg || '';
  await wait(Math.max(260, t + 120));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ flow
async function doAction(a, o = {}) {
  if (!G || G.stopped) return false;
  const s = G.s;
  const pre = capture();
  const r = applyAction(s, a);
  if (!r.ok) { sfx('error'); toast(r.reason || 'Not allowed right now'); return false; }
  G.sel = [];
  G.busy = true;
  G.freshDelay = 260;
  G.noFly = !!o.noFly; // a dragged card is already where it was dropped
  await present(r.ev, pre);
  if (G) G.noFly = false;
  if (!G || G.stopped) return true;
  G.busy = false;
  after();
  return true;
}

function after() {
  const s = G.s;
  if (s.over) return finish();
  if (s.players[G.me].out) return finish();
  nextStep();
}

function nextStep() {
  if (!G || G.stopped || G.paused || G.busy) return;
  const s = G.s;
  renderAll();
  clearTimeout(G.botT);
  if (s.over) return;
  if (s.cur === G.me) beginHuman();
  else { clearInterval(G.tmr); G.botT = setTimeout(botMove, 650 + Math.random() * 600); }
}

function botMove() {
  if (!G || G.stopped || G.paused || G.busy) return;
  const s = G.s;
  if (s.over) return;
  const p = s.players[s.cur];
  if (!p.bot) return;
  let a;
  try { a = chooseAction(s, p.bot.L); } catch (e) { a = null; }
  if (!a) { const sm = E.summary(s); a = sm.canTake ? { type: 'take' } : sm.canAccept ? { type: 'accept' } : { type: 'pass' }; }
  doAction(a).then((ok) => {
    if (!ok && G && !G.stopped) { const sm = E.summary(G.s); doAction(sm.canTake ? { type: 'take' } : sm.canAccept ? { type: 'accept' } : { type: 'pass' }); }
  });
}

// ---- human turn + one-minute timer (spec: 5 stars, lose one on timeout, out at zero)
function beginHuman() {
  const key = G.s.turnNo;
  if (G.turnKey !== key) { G.turnKey = key; G.deadline = Date.now() + 60000; G.lastSec = 99; }
  clearInterval(G.tmr);
  if (store.get().settings.timer) { G.tmr = setInterval(tick, 200); tick(); }
}

function tick() {
  if (!G || G.stopped || G.paused) return;
  const s = G.s;
  if (s.over || s.cur !== G.me) return clearInterval(G.tmr);
  // nothing in hand fits: no countdown, nothing happens on its own. The player takes a card when ready.
  if (!G.sel.length && !E.playableIds(s).size) {
    G.deadline = Date.now() + 60000;
    const b0 = $('#mine .tbar i'), p0 = $('#mine .plate');
    if (b0) b0.style.transform = 'scaleX(1)';
    if (p0) p0.classList.remove('low');
    return;
  }
  const left = G.deadline - Date.now();
  const bar = $('#mine .tbar i'), plate = $('#mine .plate');
  if (bar) bar.style.transform = `scaleX(${Math.max(0, left / 60000)})`;
  if (plate) plate.classList.toggle('low', left < 15000);
  const sec = Math.ceil(left / 1000);
  if (sec <= 10 && sec !== G.lastSec && sec > 0) { G.lastSec = sec; sfx('tick'); }
  if (left <= 0 && !G.busy) humanTimeout();
}

async function humanTimeout() {
  clearInterval(G.tmr);
  closeSheet(true);
  G.sel = [];
  G.busy = true;
  const pre = capture();
  const r = E.timeout(G.s);
  toast('Time is up! You lose a star', 2200);
  await present(r.ev || [], pre);
  if (!G || G.stopped) return;
  G.busy = false;
  after();
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ input
const sameGroup = (a, b) => (E.cat(a) === 'P' && E.cat(b) === 'P') || (E.cat(a) === E.cat(b) && E.cat(a) !== 'P' && E.rc(a) === E.rc(b));

function onCard(id) {
  if (!G || G.busy || G.paused || G.s.over || G.s.cur !== G.me) return;
  const s = G.s;
  if (G.sel.includes(id)) { sfx('click'); if (G.sel.length === 1 || true) return tryPlay(); }
  let next;
  if (G.sel.length && sameGroup(G.sel[0], id)) next = [...G.sel, id];
  else {
    const a = E.analyze(s, [id]);
    // a card that is not playable alone may still be legal after a partner; only block when nothing could start it
    if (!a.ok && !startsSomething(id)) { sfx('error'); toast(a.reason); return; }
    next = [id];
  }
  sfx('select');
  G.sel = next;
  renderHand();
  renderBar();
}
const startsSomething = (id) => E.analyze(G.s, [id]).ok;
const canAct = () => !!G && !G.busy && !G.paused && !G.s.over && G.s.cur === G.me;

// dropped a card on the table: add it to the cards already chosen (same group) and play
function dragPlay(id) {
  if (!canAct()) return;
  let next;
  if (G.sel.includes(id)) next = G.sel;
  else if (G.sel.length && sameGroup(G.sel[0], id)) next = [...G.sel, id];
  else {
    const a = E.analyze(G.s, [id]);
    if (!a.ok) { sfx('error'); toast(a.reason); renderHand(); return; }
    next = [id];
  }
  G.sel = next;
  renderHand();
  tryPlay(true);
}

async function onAct(a, o = {}) {
  if (!G || G.busy || G.paused) return;
  sfx('click');
  if (a === 'play') return tryPlay();
  if (a === 'declare' && !E.summary(G.s).canDeclare) {
    sfx('error');
    toast(G.s.players[G.me].declared != null ? 'Niko Kadi is already called' : 'Niko Kadi only works with a winning hand');
    return;
  }
  if (a === 'take') {
    const sm = E.summary(G.s), p = G.s.players[G.me];
    if (p.hand.length + sm.takeCount > E.HAND_LIMIT) return confirmOut(() => doAction({ type: 'take' }));
    return doAction({ type: 'take' }, o);
  }
  return doAction({ type: a });
}

function confirmOut(go2) {
  G.paused = true;
  clearInterval(G.tmr);
  const pausedAt = Date.now();
  const sh = sheet(`<h2>That puts you out</h2><p>Taking these cards takes you past the 8-card limit. Try an Ace or another card to answer instead.</p><div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="n">Go back</button><button class="btn danger" id="y">Take anyway</button></div>`, { center: true, dismiss: false });
  const resume = () => { G.paused = false; G.deadline += Date.now() - pausedAt; };
  $('#n', sh.el).onclick = () => { closeSheet(true); resume(); nextStep(); };
  $('#y', sh.el).onclick = () => { closeSheet(true); resume(); go2(); };
}

async function tryPlay(viaDrag = false) {
  const s = G.s;
  if (!G.sel.length) return;
  const order = E.findOrder(s, G.sel) || G.sel.slice();
  const a = E.analyze(s, order);
  if (!a.ok) { sfx('error'); toast(a.reason); return; }
  const opts = {};
  if (a.kind === 'A') {
    G.busy = true; // block taps while a picker is open
    let mode = 'cancel';
    if (a.modes.length > 1) {
      mode = await ask('Ace on a penalty', [
        ['cancel', 'Cancel the penalty', 'Nobody draws. Play carries on.'],
        ['forward', 'Pass it on (Flow)', 'The next player takes the penalty or answers it.'],
      ]);
      if (!mode) { G.busy = false; return; }
    }
    opts.mode = mode;
    const a2 = E.analyze(s, order, { mode });
    if (!a2.ok) { G.busy = false; sfx('error'); toast(a2.reason); return; }
    if (a2.needs === 'suit') {
      const su = await pickSuit();
      if (su == null) { G.busy = false; return; }
      opts.demand = { suit: su };
    } else if (a2.needs === 'card') {
      const dm = await pickCard();
      if (!dm) { G.busy = false; return; }
      opts.demand = dm;
    }
    G.busy = false;
  }
  doAction({ type: 'place', cards: order, opts }, { noFly: viaDrag });
}

// ---- pickers
function pause() { G.paused = true; clearInterval(G.tmr); G.pausedAt = Date.now(); }
function unpause() { if (!G) return; G.paused = false; if (G.pausedAt) G.deadline += Date.now() - G.pausedAt; G.pausedAt = 0; }

function ask(title, opts) {
  return new Promise((res) => {
    let done = false;
    const sh = sheet(`<h2>${title}</h2>${opts.map(([k, t, sub]) => `<button class="opt" data-k="${k}"><span class="ic">${ICON.ace || ICON.spade}</span><div>${t}<small>${sub}</small></div></button>`).join('')}<div class="row"><button class="btn grey block" id="x">Cancel</button></div>`, { center: true, onClose: () => { if (!done) res(null); } });
    $$('.opt', sh.el).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { done = true; closeSheet(true); res(b.dataset.k); }));
    $('#x', sh.el).onclick = () => closeSheet();
  });
}

function pickSuit() {
  return new Promise((res) => {
    let done = false;
    const sh = sheet(`<h2>Which suit?</h2><p>The next player must follow it, or cancel with an Ace.</p>
      <div class="pickgrid">${[0, 1, 2, 3].map((x) => `<button class="pick ${x === 1 || x === 2 ? 'red' : ''}" data-s="${x}">${SUIT_TXT[x]}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="row"><button class="btn grey block" id="x">Cancel</button></div>`, { center: true, onClose: () => { if (!done) res(null); } });
    $$('.pick', sh.el).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { done = true; sfx('select'); closeSheet(true); res(+b.dataset.s); }));
    $('#x', sh.el).onclick = () => closeSheet();
  });
}

function pickCard() {
  return new Promise((res) => {
    let done = false, rank = null, suit = null;
    const sh = sheet(`<h2>Request a card</h2><p>Everyone draws until it is played, or an Ace cancels it.</p>
      <div class="pickgrid six">${E.NORMAL_RANKS.map((r) => `<button class="pick num" data-r="${r}">${r}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="pickgrid">${[0, 1, 2, 3].map((x) => `<button class="pick ${x === 1 || x === 2 ? 'red' : ''}" data-s="${x}">${SUIT_TXT[x]}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="x">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="ok" disabled>Request</button></div>`, { center: true, onClose: () => { if (!done) res(null); } });
    const upd = () => { $('#ok', sh.el).disabled = rank == null || suit == null; };
    $$('[data-r]', sh.el).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { rank = +b.dataset.r; sfx('select'); $$('[data-r]', sh.el).forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('on', x === b)); upd(); }));
    $$('[data-s]', sh.el).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { suit = +b.dataset.s; sfx('select'); $$('[data-s]', sh.el).forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('on', x === b)); upd(); }));
    $('#ok', sh.el).onclick = () => { done = true; closeSheet(true); res({ rank, suit }); };
    $('#x', sh.el).onclick = () => closeSheet();
  });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ menu, result
function openMenu() {
  if (!G || G.stopped || G.s.over) return;
  if (document.querySelector('#overlay:not([hidden])')) return closeSheet();
  pause();
  menuSheet({
    helpHTML: HELP_NK,
    onResume: () => { unpause(); nextStep(); },
    onResign: () => { unpause(); finish('resign'); },
  });
}

function finish(why) {
  if (!G || G.finished) return;
  G.finished = true;
  clearInterval(G.tmr);
  clearTimeout(G.botT);
  const s = G.s, me = s.players[G.me];
  const won = !!s.over && s.over.winner === G.me && why !== 'resign';
  const w = s.over ? s.players[s.over.winner] : null;
  const r = store.recordResult('nk', won, G.rivals, G.botId, { winner: won ? me.name : (w && w.name) || '', rules: G.rules });
  let title, sub = '';
  if (why === 'resign') { title = 'You left the game'; }
  else if (won) title = 'Victory! You won';
  else if (me.out && !s.over) { title = "You're out"; sub = me.outReason === 'limit' ? 'Over the 8-card limit.' : 'Out of stars.'; }
  else title = `Defeat! ${esc(w.name)} won`;
  const winner = won ? me : w;
  const rk = rankInfo(r.rp);
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (!G || G.stopped) return;
    G.busy = true;
    $('#bar').innerHTML = '';
    const sh = sheet(`<div class="result ${won ? 'won' : 'lost'}">
      <span class="ic trophy">${won ? ICON.trophy : ICON.spade}</span>
      <h2>${title}</h2>${sub ? `<p>${sub}</p>` : ''}
      ${winner ? `<div class="who">${avatarHTML(winner.avatar, 56)}</div>` : ''}
      <div class="delta ${r.delta >= 0 ? 'up' : 'down'}">${r.delta > 0 ? '+' : ''}${r.delta} RP</div>
      <p class="rk">${rk.name} &middot; ${r.rp} RP${r.rankUp ? ` &middot; <b>Promoted to ${r.rankUp}!</b>` : ''}</p>
      <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="rHome">Home</button><button class="btn" id="rAgain">Rematch</button></div></div>`, { center: true, dismiss: false });
    $('#rHome', sh.el).onclick = () => { sfx('click'); closeSheet(true); leave(); };
    $('#rAgain', sh.el).onclick = () => { sfx('click'); closeSheet(true); const { botId, count, rules } = G; startNiko({ botId, count, rules }); };
  }, why === 'resign' ? 0 : 900);
}

function leave() {
  stopNiko();
  document.body.classList.remove('playing');
  go('home');
}

=== www/js/playseq.js ===
// Special Sequence table.
import * as E from './engine.js';
import { newSeqGame, seqPick, seqDispose, SEQ_PATTERNS } from './sequence.js';
import { BOTS, pickOpponents, seqChoosePick, seqChooseDispose } from './bots.js';
import { cardEl, backEl, wait } from './cards.js';
import { playerAvatar, avatarHTML } from './avatars.js';
import { ICON } from './icons.js';
import { sfx, configure } from './sound.js';
import * as store from './store.js';
import { $, $$, esc, toast, sheet, closeSheet } from './ui.js';
import { go, pushBack } from './router.js';
import { SLOTS, fly, flyBack, plateHTML, fanHTML, layoutHand, menuSheet, dragify, HELP_SS } from './table.js';

let G = null;

export function stopSeq() {
  if (!G) return;
  G.stopped = true;
  clearTimeout(G.botT);
  G.unback && G.unback();
  G = null;
}

export function startSeq({ botId, count }) {
  stopSeq();
  const d = store.get();
  const opp = pickOpponents(botId, count - 1);
  const list = [{ name: d.name, avatar: playerAvatar(d), human: true }, ...opp.map((b) => ({ name: b.name, bot: b, avatar: b.avatar }))];
  const players = list.map((p) => ({ p, k: Math.random() })).sort((a, b) => a.k - b.k).map((x) => x.p);
  const s = newSeqGame({ players });
  G = { s, me: players.findIndex((p) => p.human), botId, count, sel: [], busy: true, paused: false, stopped: false, botT: 0, msg: '', els: new Map(), rivals: opp };
  store.get().lastBot.ss = botId;
  document.body.classList.add('playing');
  G.unback = pushBack(() => openMenu());
  build();
  renderAll(true);
  sfx('shuffle');
  setTimeout(() => sfx('deal'), 350);
  wait(900).then(() => { if (!G || G.stopped) return; G.busy = false; nextStep(); });
}

function build() {
  $('#screen').innerHTML = `<div class="table seq" id="tbl">
    <button class="hint" id="snd" aria-label="Sound"></button>
    <button class="menu" id="menu" aria-label="Menu"><span class="ic">${ICON.list}</span></button>
    <div id="seats"></div>
    <div class="center"><div class="pile tap" id="deadP"></div><div class="pile" id="dispP"></div></div>
    <div class="goal">${Object.values(SEQ_PATTERNS).map((t) => `<span>${t}</span>`).join('')}</div>
    <div class="status" id="status"></div>
    <div class="mine" id="mine"></div>
    <div class="bar" id="bar"></div>
    <div class="hand" id="hand"></div>
  </div>`;
  $('#menu').onclick = () => { sfx('click'); openMenu(); };
  $('#snd').onclick = () => { const d = store.get(); d.settings.sound = !d.settings.sound; store.save(); configure({ enabled: d.settings.sound }); renderSnd(); if (d.settings.sound) sfx('click'); };
  const topOf = (sel) => { const c = [...document.querySelectorAll(sel + ' .card')].pop(); return c ? c.getBoundingClientRect() : null; };
  const pileDrag = (id, from, ghost, sel) => dragify($(id), {
    can: () => myTurn() && !G.busy && !G.paused && G.s.phase === 'pick' && (from === 'dead' ? G.s.dead.length : G.s.dispose.length) > 0,
    ghost, rectOf: () => topOf(sel),
    hit: (x, y) => y > $('#hand').getBoundingClientRect().top - 50,
    hi: () => $('#hand'),
    target: () => $('#hand').getBoundingClientRect(),
    onDrop: () => onPile(from, true),
    onTap: () => onPile(from),
  });
  pileDrag('#deadP', 'dead', () => backEl(), '#deadP');
  pileDrag('#dispP', 'dispose', () => cardEl(G.s.dispose[G.s.dispose.length - 1]), '#dispP');
  renderSnd();
}
const renderSnd = () => { const b = $('#snd'); if (b) b.innerHTML = `<span class="ic">${store.get().settings.sound ? ICON.sound : ICON.mute}</span>`; };

const nm = (i) => (i === G.me ? 'You' : G.s.players[i].name);
const seatRect = (i) => { const el = $(`.plate[data-p="${i}"]`); return el ? el.getBoundingClientRect() : null; };
const myTurn = () => !!G && !G.s.over && G.s.cur === G.me;

function renderAll(initial = false) {
  const s = G.s, n = s.n, slots = SLOTS[n];
  let html = '';
  for (let k = 1; k < n; k++) {
    const i = (G.me + k) % n, p = s.players[i];
    html += `<div class="seat ${slots[k - 1]}">${plateHTML({ ...p, stars: null }, i, { active: s.cur === i && !s.over })}${fanHTML(p.hand.length)}</div>`;
  }
  $('#seats').innerHTML = html;
  $('#mine').innerHTML = plateHTML({ ...s.players[G.me], stars: null }, G.me, { active: myTurn() });
  renderPiles();
  renderStatus();
  renderHand(initial);
  renderBar();
}

function renderPiles() {
  const s = G.s, pick = myTurn() && s.phase === 'pick';
  const dp = $('#deadP');
  dp.className = 'pile tap' + (pick ? ' glow' : '') + (s.dead.length ? '' : ' empty');
  let h = '';
  const nb = Math.min(3, s.dead.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < nb; i++) { const b = backEl(); b.style.transform = `translate(${(i - nb + 1) * 2}px,${(i - nb + 1) * 2}px)`; h += b.outerHTML; }
  dp.innerHTML = h + `<span class="cnt">${s.dead.length}</span><span class="lbl">Dead pile</span>`;
  const sp = $('#dispP');
  sp.className = 'pile' + (s.dispose.length ? ' tap' : ' empty') + (pick && s.dispose.length ? ' glow' : '');
  sp.innerHTML = '';
  s.dispose.slice(-3).forEach((id, i, arr) => {
    const el = cardEl(id);
    const depth = arr.length - 1 - i;
    el.style.transform = `rotate(${depth === 0 ? 0 : (((id * 37) % 11) - 5) * 1.3}deg) translate(${depth * -3}px,${depth * -2}px)`;
    sp.appendChild(el);
  });
  const lb = document.createElement('span');
  lb.className = 'lbl';
  lb.textContent = 'Dispose';
  sp.appendChild(lb);
}

function renderStatus() {
  const s = G.s, el = $('#status');
  if (s.over) { el.hidden = true; return; }
  el.hidden = false;
  if (s.cur === G.me) { el.className = 'status you'; el.textContent = s.phase === 'pick' ? 'Your turn · take a card from either pile' : 'Now throw away one card'; }
  else { el.className = 'status'; el.textContent = G.msg || `${s.players[s.cur].name} is thinking…`; }
}

const byRank = (h) => h.slice().sort((a, b) => E.orderOf(a) - E.orderOf(b) || E.suitOf(a) - E.suitOf(b));

function renderHand(initial) {
  const s = G.s, box = $('#hand');
  const ids = byRank(s.players[G.me].hand);
  for (const [id, el] of G.els) if (!ids.includes(id)) { el.remove(); G.els.delete(id); }
  const els = ids.map((id) => {
    let el = G.els.get(id);
    if (!el) {
      el = cardEl(id);
      dragify(el, {
        can: () => !!G && myTurn() && !G.busy && !G.paused && G.s.phase === 'dispose',
        ghost: () => { const g = el.cloneNode(true); g.classList.remove('sel', 'dragging'); return g; },
        hit: (x, y) => y < $('#hand').getBoundingClientRect().top - 12,
        hi: () => $('.center'),
        target: () => $('#dispP').getBoundingClientRect(),
        onDrop: () => { G.sel = [id]; throwAway(true); },
        onTap: () => onCard(id),
      });
      G.els.set(id, el);
      el.dataset.fresh = '1';
    }
    el.classList.toggle('sel', G.sel.includes(id));
    box.appendChild(el);
    return el;
  });
  layoutHand(box, els);
  const from = (G.lastPile && $(G.lastPile === 'dispose' ? '#dispP' : '#deadP').getBoundingClientRect()) || $('#deadP').getBoundingClientRect();
  els.forEach((el, i) => { if (el.dataset.fresh) { delete el.dataset.fresh; { if (!G.noFly) fly(el, from, { delay: initial ? 120 + i * 120 : 0 }); } } });
}

function renderBar() {
  const s = G.s, bar = $('#bar');
  if (!myTurn() || G.busy || G.paused || s.phase !== 'dispose') { bar.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  bar.innerHTML = G.sel.length ? `<button class="act go" data-a="dispose">Throw away</button>` : '';
  const b = $('.act', bar);
  if (b) b.onclick = () => { sfx('click'); throwAway(); };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ actions
function onCard(id) {
  if (!G || G.busy || G.paused || !myTurn() || G.s.phase !== 'dispose') return;
  if (G.sel[0] === id) { sfx('click'); return throwAway(); }
  sfx('select');
  G.sel = [id];
  renderHand();
  renderBar();
}

function onPile(from, viaDrag = false) {
  if (!G || G.busy || G.paused || !myTurn()) return;
  const s = G.s;
  if (s.phase !== 'pick') { toast('Throw away a card first'); sfx('error'); return; }
  if (from === 'dispose' && !s.dispose.length) { toast('Nothing in the dispose pile yet'); sfx('error'); return; }
  act(() => seqPick(s, from), { from, noFly: viaDrag });
}

function throwAway(viaDrag = false) {
  if (!G.sel.length) return;
  const c = G.sel[0];
  const rect = G.els.get(c) ? G.els.get(c).getBoundingClientRect() : null;
  act(() => seqDispose(G.s, c), { rect, card: c, noFly: viaDrag });
}

async function act(fn, ctx = {}) {
  const s = G.s;
  const pre = { dead: $('#deadP').getBoundingClientRect(), disp: $('#dispP').getBoundingClientRect() };
  const r = fn();
  if (!r.ok) { sfx('error'); toast(r.reason || 'Not allowed'); return; }
  G.sel = [];
  G.busy = true;
  G.noFly = !!ctx.noFly;
  await present(r.ev, pre, ctx);
  if (G) G.noFly = false;
  if (!G || G.stopped) return;
  G.busy = false;
  if (s.over) return finish();
  nextStep();
}

async function present(ev, pre, ctx) {
  const s = G.s;
  const lastPick = ev.find((e) => e.t === 'pick');
  G.lastPile = lastPick ? lastPick.from : null;
  renderAll();
  let t = 0;
  const at = (ms, fn) => setTimeout(() => { if (G && !G.stopped) fn(); }, ms);
  const msgs = [];
  for (const e of ev) {
    switch (e.t) {
      case 'pick': {
        at(t, () => sfx(e.from === 'dispose' ? 'flip' : 'draw'));
        if (e.p !== G.me) { flyBack(e.from === 'dispose' ? pre.disp : pre.dead, seatRect(e.p), { delay: t }); msgs.push(`${nm(e.p)} took from the ${e.from === 'dispose' ? 'dispose' : 'dead'} pile`); }
        t += 260;
        break;
      }
      case 'dispose': {
        at(t, () => sfx('place'));
        const node = $(`#dispP .card[data-id="${e.card}"]`);
        const from = e.p === G.me ? ctx.rect : seatRect(e.p);
        if (node && from && !(G.noFly && e.p === G.me)) fly(node, from, { delay: t });
        if (e.p !== G.me) msgs.push(`${nm(e.p)} threw away ${E.cardName(e.card)}`);
        t += 260;
        break;
      }
      case 'refill': at(t, () => sfx('shuffle')); t += 350; break;
      case 'turn': if (e.p === G.me) at(t + 60, () => sfx('turn')); break;
      case 'win': at(t + 150, () => sfx(e.p === G.me ? 'win' : 'lose')); t += 500; break;
    }
  }
  if (msgs.length) G.msg = msgs.slice(-1)[0];
  if (G.s.cur !== G.me && !G.s.over) { const st = $('#status'); if (st) st.textContent = G.msg; }
  await wait(Math.max(240, t + 100));
}

function nextStep() {
  if (!G || G.stopped || G.paused || G.busy) return;
  const s = G.s;
  renderAll();
  clearTimeout(G.botT);
  if (s.over || s.cur === G.me) return;
  G.botT = setTimeout(botMove, 700 + Math.random() * 500);
}

function botMove() {
  if (!G || G.stopped || G.paused || G.busy) return;
  const s = G.s;
  if (s.over || s.cur === G.me) return;
  const L = s.players[s.cur].bot.L;
  if (s.phase === 'pick') act(() => seqPick(s, seqChoosePick(s, L)));
  else { const c = seqChooseDispose(s, L); const rect = seatRect(s.cur); act(() => seqDispose(s, c), { rect, card: c }); }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ menu, result
function openMenu() {
  if (!G || G.stopped || G.s.over) return;
  if (document.querySelector('#overlay:not([hidden])')) return closeSheet();
  G.paused = true;
  clearTimeout(G.botT);
  menuSheet({
    helpHTML: HELP_SS,
    onResume: () => { if (!G) return; G.paused = false; nextStep(); },
    onResign: () => { G.paused = false; finish('resign'); },
  });
}

function finish(why) {
  if (!G || G.finished) return;
  G.finished = true;
  clearTimeout(G.botT);
  const s = G.s;
  const won = !!s.over && s.over.winner === G.me && why !== 'resign';
  const w = s.over ? s.players[s.over.winner] : null;
  store.recordResult('ss', won, G.rivals, G.botId, { winner: won ? s.players[G.me].name : (w && w.name) || '' });
  const title = why === 'resign' ? 'You left the game' : won ? 'Victory! You won' : `Defeat! ${esc(w.name)} won`;
  const sub = why === 'resign' ? '' : SEQ_PATTERNS[s.over.pattern];
  const reveal = w && why !== 'resign' ? `<div class="reveal">${w.hand.slice().sort((a, b) => E.orderOf(a) - E.orderOf(b)).map((c) => cardEl(c).outerHTML).join('')}</div>` : '';
  const st = store.get().stats.ss;
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (!G || G.stopped) return;
    G.busy = true;
    $('#bar').innerHTML = '';
    const sh = sheet(`<div class="result ${won ? 'won' : 'lost'}">
      <span class="ic trophy">${won ? ICON.trophy : ICON.seq}</span>
      <h2>${title}</h2><p>${sub}</p>${reveal}
      <p class="rk">Special Sequence wins: ${st.won} of ${st.played}</p>
      <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="rHome">Home</button><button class="btn" id="rAgain">Rematch</button></div></div>`, { center: true, dismiss: false });
    $('#rHome', sh.el).onclick = () => { sfx('click'); closeSheet(true); leave(); };
    $('#rAgain', sh.el).onclick = () => { sfx('click'); closeSheet(true); const { botId, count } = G; startSeq({ botId, count }); };
  }, why === 'resign' ? 0 : 900);
}

function leave() {
  stopSeq();
  document.body.classList.remove('playing');
  go('home');
}

=== www/js/router.js ===
// Tiny indirection so screens, the table and main.js can navigate without importing each other.
let impl = { go() {}, start() {} };
export const setRouter = (o) => Object.assign(impl, o);
export const go = (tab) => impl.go(tab);
export const startGame = (game, opts) => impl.start(game, opts);

// Back-button stack: the innermost handler gets the Android back press first.
const stack = [];
export const pushBack = (fn) => { stack.push(fn); return () => { const i = stack.indexOf(fn); if (i >= 0) stack.splice(i, 1); }; };
export const handleBack = () => { const fn = stack[stack.length - 1]; if (fn) { fn(); return true; } return false; };

=== www/js/rp.js ===
// Rank Points (RP). How a match moves your rank, and the rank titles.
//
// Why not +4/-3, +3/-2, +1/-1?  Played between equal players those numbers lose RP on average
// (about -1.25 a game at a four-player table), and they ignore who you beat. So:
//   - the winner takes 2 RP from each rival, and each loser pays 2 RP (zero-sum between equals);
//   - the pay is scaled by the strength of the rivals: beating Hard or Expert players is worth
//     more, and losing to them costs less; beating Easy players is worth less, and losing costs more.
// When online play arrives the same scale can use the rivals' own RP instead of a level.
export const TIER_W = { Easy: 0.6, Medium: 1, Hard: 1.4, Expert: 1.8 };

export const RANKS = [
  ['Rookie', 0], ['Regular', 40], ['Skilled', 100], ['Veteran', 200], ['Master', 350], ['Legend', 550],
];

export function rpDelta(won, n, rivals) {
  const s = rivals.length ? rivals.reduce((a, b) => a + (TIER_W[b.tier] || 1), 0) / rivals.length : 1;
  return won ? Math.max(1, Math.round(2 * (n - 1) * s)) : -Math.max(1, Math.round(2 / s));
}

export function rankInfo(rp) {
  let i = 0;
  while (i + 1 < RANKS.length && rp >= RANKS[i + 1][1]) i++;
  const [name, base] = RANKS[i];
  const nxt = RANKS[i + 1] || null;
  return { name, idx: i, base, next: nxt ? nxt[0] : null, nextAt: nxt ? nxt[1] : null, pct: nxt ? Math.round(((rp - base) / (nxt[1] - base)) * 100) : 100 };
}

export function applyTheme() {
  const d = store.get();
  const S = d.settings;
  const b = document.body;
  b.dataset.back = S.back;
  b.dataset.felt = S.felt;
  b.dataset.mode = S.mode === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';
  b.dataset.theme = THEMES[S.theme] ? S.theme : 'parlour';
  b.classList.toggle('reduce-motion', !!S.reduceMotion);
  configure({ enabled: S.sound, volume: S.volume, haptics: S.haptics });
  const m = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
  if (m) m.content = S.mode === 'dark' ? '#1b2026' : '#e9dfc6';
}

const myAvatar = () => playerAvatar(store.get());
const winRate = (s) => (s.played ? Math.round((s.won / s.played) * 100) + '%' : '–');

function ago(t) {
  const d = new Date(t), now = new Date();
  const day = (x) => new Date(x.getFullYear(), x.getMonth(), x.getDate()).getTime();
  const diff = Math.round((day(now) - day(d)) / 864e5);
  const hm = d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
  if (diff <= 0) return 'Today, ' + hm;
  if (diff === 1) return 'Yesterday, ' + hm;
  return d.toLocaleDateString([], { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ art
function heroArt(g) {
  const ids = g === 'nk' ? ['AS', 'KH', 'QD'] : ['4C', '5H', '6S', '7D'];
  const pose = g === 'nk' ? [[-14, 8, 4], [0, 2, 34], [14, 8, 64]] : [[-15, 10, 0], [-5, 4, 26], [5, 2, 52], [15, 8, 78]];
  const w = 46;
  return ids.map((s, i) => {
    const el = cardEl(cardId(s));
    el.style.setProperty('--cw', w + 'px');
    el.style.left = pose[i][2] + 'px';
    el.style.top = pose[i][1] + 'px';
    el.style.transform = `rotate(${pose[i][0]}deg)`;
    return el.outerHTML;
  }).join('');
}

function gamePanel(g) {
  const d = store.get();
  const meta = g === 'nk' ? `${rankInfo(d.rp).name} · ${d.rp} RP` : 'Casual · no rank points';
  return `<button class="game ${g}" data-g="${g}" aria-label="Play ${GAMES[g].name}">
    <div class="art">${heroArt(g)}</div>
    <h2>${GAMES[g].name}</h2><span class="sub">${GAMES[g].tag}</span>
    <span class="meta">${meta}</span>
    <span class="go"><span class="ic">${ICON.play}</span>Play</span>
  </button>`;
}

function recentHTML() {
  const d = store.get();
  const m = d.history[0];
  if (!m) return `<div class="recent empty">No matches yet. Your latest game will show up here.</div>`;
  const rivals = m.names.map((n) => BOTS.find((b) => b.name === n)).filter(Boolean);
  const faces = rivals.slice(0, 3).map((b) => avatarHTML(b.avatar, 46)).join('');
  const rp = m.g === 'nk' && m.delta ? ` · ${m.delta > 0 ? '+' : ''}${m.delta} RP` : '';
  return `<div class="recent">
    <div class="faces">${faces}</div>
    <div class="info"><b>${esc(m.names.join(', '))}</b>
      <span><i class="res ${m.won ? 'w' : 'l'}">${m.won ? 'WON' : 'LOST'}</i>${GAMES[m.g].name} · ${m.count} players${rp}</span>
      <span>${ago(m.t)}</span></div>
    <button class="again" id="again" aria-label="Rematch"><span class="ic">${ICON.rematch}</span></button>
  </div>`;
}

export function renderHome(root) {
  const d = store.get();
  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

      <button class="icon-btn" id="bell" aria-label="Notifications"><span class="ic">${ICON.bell}</span><span class="badge" ${note ? '' : 'hidden'}>${note}</span></button>
    </div>
    <div class="rankbar" id="rankbar">
      <div class="medal"><span class="ic">${ICON.trophy}</span></div>
      <div class="rk"><small>Rank</small><b>${ri.name}</b><div class="bar-rp"><i style="width:${Math.max(4, ri.pct)}%"></i></div><span class="nx">${nx}</span></div>

    </div>
    <div class="sec"><span>Pick a game</span></div>
    <div class="games">${['nk', 'ss'].map(gamePanel).join('')}</div>
    <div class="sec"><span>Recent match</span></div>
    <div id="recent">${recentHTML()}</div>
  </div>`;

  $('#meBtn', root).onclick = () => { go('more'); };
  $('#bell', root).onclick = () => { showInbox('note', () => renderHome(root)); };
  $('#rankbar', root).onclick = () => { go('rank'); };
  $$('.game', root).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { showModes(b.dataset.g, { computer: showSetup }); }));
  const again = $('#again', root);
  if (again) again.onclick = () => {
    const m = store.get().history[0];
    startGame(m.g, { botId: m.botId, count: m.count, rules: m.rules || d.rules });

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ avatar picker (faces, things, own photo)
function pickerHTML() {
  return `<div class="av-now" id="avNow"></div>
    <div class="seg" id="avTabs"><button data-t="faces">Faces</button><button data-t="things">Things</button><button data-t="photo">Photo</button></div>
    <div id="avBody"></div>`;
}
function resizePhoto(file) {
  return new Promise((res, rej) => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = () => {
      const S = 192, c = document.createElement('canvas');
      c.width = c.height = S;
      const m = Math.min(img.width, img.height);
      c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, (img.width - m) / 2, (img.height - m) / 2, m, m, 0, 0, S, S);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      res(c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85));
    };
    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); rej(new Error('bad image')); };
    img.src = url;
  });
}
// st = { av, photo }; edits it in place.
function bindPicker(el, st) {
  let tab = st.av === 'photo' ? 'photo' : typeof st.av === 'string' ? 'things' : 'faces';
  const cur = () => (st.av === 'photo' && st.photo ? { img: st.photo } : typeof st.av === 'string' && OBJECT_LIST.some((o) => o.id === st.av) ? { obj: st.av } : PLAYER_AVATARS[st.av] || PLAYER_AVATARS[0]);
  const draw = () => {
    $('#avNow', el).innerHTML = avatarHTML(cur(), 76);
    $$('#avTabs button', el).forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.t === tab));
    const body = $('#avBody', el);
    if (tab === 'faces') body.innerHTML = `<div class="avgrid">${PLAYER_AVATARS.map((a, i) => `<button data-v="${i}" class="${i === st.av ? 'on' : ''}">${avatarHTML(a, 60)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
    else if (tab === 'things') body.innerHTML = `<div class="avgrid">${OBJECT_LIST.map((o) => `<button data-v="${o.id}" class="${o.id === st.av ? 'on' : ''}" aria-label="${o.name}">${avatarHTML({ obj: o.id }, 60)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
    else body.innerHTML = `<div class="photo-box">${st.photo ? `<button class="usephoto ${st.av === 'photo' ? 'on' : ''}" data-v="photo">${avatarHTML({ img: st.photo }, 84)}</button>` : '<p>Use a picture from your phone. It is cropped to a square and stays on this device.</p>'}
      <div class="row"><label class="btn small grey" for="avFile">${st.photo ? 'Choose another' : 'Upload a photo'}</label>${st.photo ? '<button class="btn small grey" id="avDel">Remove</button>' : ''}</div>
      <input type="file" id="avFile" accept="image/*" hidden></div>`;
    $$('[data-v]', body).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { const v = b.dataset.v; st.av = /^\d+$/.test(v) ? +v : v; draw(); }));
    const f = $('#avFile', body);
    if (f) f.onchange = async () => {
      if (!f.files[0]) return;
      try { st.photo = await resizePhoto(f.files[0]); st.av = 'photo'; } catch (e) { toast('That file could not be read as an image'); }
      draw();
    };
    const del = $('#avDel', body);
    if (del) del.onclick = () => { st.photo = ''; if (st.av === 'photo') st.av = 0; draw(); };
  };
  $$('#avTabs button', el).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { tab = b.dataset.t; draw(); }));
  draw();
}

function welcome() {
  const d = store.get();
  if (d.welcomed) return;
  let name = d.name === 'You' ? '' : d.name;
  const st = { av: d.avatar, photo: d.photo || '' };
  const sh = sheet(`
    <h2>Welcome!</h2>
    <p>What should the table call you?</p>
    <div style="margin-top:14px"><input type="text" id="wName" maxlength="14" placeholder="Your name" value="${esc(name)}" autocomplete="off"></div>
    <h3>Pick your picture</h3>
    ${pickerHTML()}
    <div class="row"><button class="btn block" id="wGo">Let's play</button></div>`, { center: true, dismiss: false });
  bindPicker(sh.el, st);
  $('#wGo', sh.el).onclick = () => {
    d.name = ($('#wName', sh.el).value || '').trim().slice(0, 14) || 'You';
    d.avatar = st.av; d.photo = st.photo;
    d.welcomed = true;
    store.save();
    closeSheet(true);
    go('home');
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ leaderboard
export function renderRank(root) {
  const d = store.get();
  const ri = rankInfo(d.rp);
  const st = d.stats.nk;
  const rows = BOTS.map((b) => ({ bot: b, r: b.lb })).concat([{ you: true, r: d.rp }]).sort((a, b) => b.r - a.r);
  root.innerHTML = `<div class="page">
    <h1>Leaderboard</h1>
    <div class="me-card">${avatarHTML(myAvatar(), 56)}<div><b>${esc(d.name)}</b><div class="sm">${ri.name} · ${st.won} won, ${st.played - st.won} lost</div></div><div class="big">${d.rp}<small>RP</small></div></div>
    <div class="sec"><span>Niko Kadi</span></div>
    <div class="board">${rows.map((x, i) => x.you
      ? `<div class="lrow you"><span class="pos">${i + 1}</span>${avatarHTML(myAvatar(), 42)}<div class="nm"><b>${esc(d.name)}</b><small>You · ${ri.name}</small></div><span class="rat">${x.r}<small>RP</small></span></div>`
      : (() => {
          const v = d.vs['nk:' + x.bot.id] || { w: 0, l: 0 };
          return `<button class="lrow" data-b="${x.bot.id}"><span class="pos">${i + 1}</span>${avatarHTML(x.bot.avatar, 42)}<div class="nm"><b>${esc(x.bot.name)}</b><small>${x.bot.tier} · you ${v.w}–${v.l}</small></div><span class="rat">${x.r}<small>RP</small></span></button>`;
        })()).join('')}</div>
    <div class="note-sm">Computer players keep a fixed RP. Beat them to climb past them.</div>
    <button class="linkbtn" id="rpHow">How Rank Points work</button>
  </div>`;
  $$('.lrow[data-b]', root).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { const dd = store.get(); dd.level.nk = BOTS.findIndex((x) => x.id === b.dataset.b); store.save(); showSetup('nk'); }));
  $('#rpHow', root).onclick = () => { info('How Rank Points work', RP_HELP); };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ more
const item = (id, icon, title, sub, pill) =>
  `<button class="item" id="${id}"><span class="ic">${ICON[icon]}</span><span class="grow">${title}${sub ? `<div class="sub">${sub}</div>` : ''}</span>${pill ? `<span class="pill">${pill}</span>` : ''}<span class="ic">${ICON.chevron}</span></button>`;

export function renderMore(root) {
  const d = store.get();
  const ri = rankInfo(d.rp);
  const um = store.unread('msg'), un = store.unread('note');
  root.innerHTML = `<div class="page">
    <h1>More</h1>
    <button class="profile" id="prof">${avatarHTML(myAvatar(), 64)}<div><b>${esc(d.name)}</b><small>${ri.name} · ${d.rp} RP</small></div><span class="ic">${ICON.chevron}</span></button>
    <div class="group">Inbox</div>
    <div class="list">${item('mMsg', 'mail', 'Messages', 'Chat with friends & Niko Kadi team', um || '')}${item('mNote', 'bell', 'Notifications', 'Results, ranks and streaks', un || '')}</div>
    <div class="group">You</div>
    <div class="list">${item('mEdit', 'user', 'Edit profile', 'Name and face')}${item('mLook', 'palette', 'Appearance', `${d.settings.mode === 'dark' ? 'Dark' : 'Light'} mode · ${THEMES[d.settings.theme][0]} theme`)}${item('mSet', 'gear', 'Settings', 'Sound, vibration, timer')}</div>
    <div class="group">Help</div>
    <div class="list">${item('mHelp', 'info', 'How to play')}${item('mRP', 'star', 'How Rank Points work')}</div>
    <div class="group">About and policies</div>
    <div class="list">${item('mAbout', 'spade', 'About Niko Kadi', 'Version 0.4')}${item('mTerms', 'doc', 'Terms of Service')}${item('mPriv', 'lock', 'Privacy Policy')}${item('mFair', 'shield', 'Fair Play Policy')}</div>
  </div>`;
  const on = (id, fn) => { $('#' + id, root).onclick = () => { fn(); }; };
  const back = () => renderMore(root);
  on('prof', () => editProfile(back));
  on('mEdit', () => editProfile(back));
  on('mMsg', () => openChats());
  on('mNote', () => showInbox('note', back));
  on('mLook', () => showAppearance(back));
  on('mSet', showSettings);
  on('mHelp', showHelp);
  on('mRP', () => info('How Rank Points work', RP_HELP));
  on('mAbout', () => info('About Niko Kadi', ABOUT));
  on('mTerms', () => info('Terms of Service', TERMS));
  on('mPriv', () => info('Privacy Policy', PRIVACY));
  on('mFair', () => info('Fair Play Policy', FAIR));
}

    <div class="row">${list.some((m) => !m.read) ? '<button class="btn grey" id="allRead">Mark all read</button>' : ''}<button class="btn" id="ok">Close</button></div>`, { onClose: done });

}

export function showSettings() {

  const row = (k, t, sub) => `<div class="toggle-row"><div class="grow"><b>${t}</b>${sub ? `<small>${sub}</small>` : ''}</div><button class="switch ${S[k] ? 'on' : ''}" data-k="${k}" role="switch" aria-checked="${!!S[k]}"></button></div>`;
  const sh = sheet(`<h2>Settings</h2>
    ${row('sound', 'Sound effects')}
    <div class="toggle-row"><div class="grow"><b>Volume</b></div><input type="range" id="vol" min="0" max="100" value="${Math.round(S.volume * 100)}"></div>
    ${row('haptics', 'Vibration', 'Small buzzes when you play')}
    ${row('reduceMotion', 'Reduce motion', 'Cards snap instead of flying')}
    ${row('timer', 'Turn timer', 'One minute a turn, five stars')}
    <div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="sReset">Reset progress</button><button class="btn" id="sOk">Done</button></div>`);
  $$('.switch', sh.el).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => {
