  }));
  $('#vol', sh.el).oninput = (e) => { S.volume = e.target.value / 100; configure({ volume: S.volume }); };
  $('#vol', sh.el).onchange = () => { store.save(); tone('place'); };
  $('#sOk', sh.el).onclick = () => { closeSheet(); };
  $('#sReset', sh.el).onclick = () => {
    const c = sheet(`<h2>Reset progress?</h2><p>Rank points, stats, matches and messages go back to the start. Your settings stay.</p><div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="n">Keep</button><button class="btn danger" id="y">Reset</button></div>`, { center: true });
    $('#n', c.el).onclick = () => closeSheet();
    $('#y', c.el).onclick = () => {
      try { localStorage.removeItem('nikokadi.v2'); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
      location.reload();
    };
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ play computer
export function showSetup(game) {
  const d = store.get();
  const screen = $('#screen');
  document.body.classList.add('playing');
  const unback = pushBack(() => leave());
  let li = d.level[game] ?? 2;
  if (!BOTS[li]) li = 2;
  let count = d.count[game] ?? 2;
  const rules = { ...d.rules };
  const leave = () => { unback(); document.body.classList.remove('playing'); go('home'); };
  const pips = (t) => `<span class="pips">${TIERS.map((_, i) => `<i class="${i <= TIERS.indexOf(t) ? 'on' : ''}"></i>`).join('')}</span>`;

  const stakes = () => {
    if (game !== 'nk') return 'Special Sequence earns no rank points.';
    const opp = pickOpponents(BOTS[li].id, count - 1);
    return `Win <b>+${rpDelta(true, count, opp)} RP</b> · Lose <b>${rpDelta(false, count, opp)} RP</b>`;
  };

  const draw = () => {
    const bot = BOTS[li];
    screen.innerHTML = `<div class="setup">
      <div class="bar-top"><button class="icon-btn" id="sBack" aria-label="Back"><span class="ic">${ICON.back}</span></button><h1>Play ${GAMES[game].name}</h1></div>
      <div class="body">
        <div class="opp-hero">${avatarHTML(bot.avatar, 84)}<div><b>${esc(bot.name)}</b><span class="lvl"><span class="tag ${bot.tier}">${bot.tier}</span>${pips(bot.tier)}</span><p>${esc(bot.blurb)}</p></div></div>
        ${TIERS.map((t) => `<div class="tier">${t}</div><div class="bots">${BOTS.map((b, i) => (b.tier === t ? `<button class="botbtn ${i === li ? 'on' : ''}" data-i="${i}">${avatarHTML(b.avatar, 44)}<div><b>${esc(b.name)}</b></div></button>` : '')).join('')}</div>`).join('')}
        <div class="tier">Table</div>
        <div class="row-set"><span>Players</span><div class="seg" id="cnt">${[2, 3, 4].map((n) => `<button data-n="${n}" class="${n === count ? 'on' : ''}">${n}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
        <div class="note-sm" id="stakes">${stakes()}</div>
        ${game === 'nk' ? `<button class="link" id="rulesBtn"><span class="grow">House rules<small>${ruleSummary(rules)}</small></span><span class="ic">${ICON.chevron}</span></button>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div class="foot"><button class="btn block" id="sStart">Start game</button></div>
    </div>`;
    $('#sBack', screen).onclick = () => { leave(); };
    $$('.botbtn', screen).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { li = +b.dataset.i; const y = $('.body', screen).scrollTop; draw(); $('.body', screen).scrollTop = y; }));
    $$('#cnt button', screen).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => { count = +b.dataset.n; $$('#cnt button', screen).forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('on', x === b)); $('#stakes', screen).innerHTML = stakes(); }));
    const rb = $('#rulesBtn', screen);
    if (rb) rb.onclick = () => { editRules(rules, () => { const y = $('.body', screen).scrollTop; draw(); $('.body', screen).scrollTop = y; }); };
    $('#sStart', screen).onclick = () => {
      d.level[game] = li; d.count[game] = count; d.rules = rules; d.game = game; store.save();
      unback();
      startGame(game, { botId: BOTS[li].id, count, rules });
    };
  };
  draw();
}

function ruleSummary(r) {
  const on = [r.flow && 'Flow', r.specialJK && 'Special J & K', r.liberal && 'Liberal'].filter(Boolean);
  return on.length ? on.join(' · ') : 'Standard rules';
}

function editRules(rules, done) {
  const items = [
    ['flow', 'Flow', 'An Ace on a penalty can pass it on instead of cancelling it.'],
    ['specialJK', 'Special J & K', 'A Jack passes a penalty forward. A King passes it back and reverses play.'],
    ['liberal', 'Liberal suits', 'Penalty cards stack on any suit.'],
  ];
  const sh = sheet(`<h2>House rules</h2><p>Only for games against the computer and private rooms. Online games always use the standard rules.</p>
    ${items.map(([k, t, s]) => `<div class="toggle-row"><div class="grow"><b>${t}</b><small>${s}</small></div><button class="switch ${rules[k] ? 'on' : ''}" data-k="${k}" role="switch" aria-checked="${!!rules[k]}"></button></div>`).join('')}
    <div class="row"><button class="btn block" id="ok">Done</button></div>`, { onClose: done });
  $$('.switch', sh.el).forEach((b) => (b.onclick = () => {
    const k = b.dataset.k;
    rules[k] = !rules[k];
    b.classList.toggle('on', rules[k]);
    b.setAttribute('aria-checked', !!rules[k]);
  }));
  $('#ok', sh.el).onclick = () => closeSheet();
}

=== www/js/sequence.js ===
// Special Sequence — rules engine.  52 cards (no jokers). Order: Ace, 2-10, King, Jack, Queen, then round to Ace again.
// A player holds 3 cards, picks one (dead pile top or dispose pile top) → 4 cards.
// If the 4 form a winning set they win; otherwise they dispose one card face-up.
import { shuffle, rand, rankOf, posOfRank, MIN_PLAYERS, MAX_PLAYERS } from './engine.js';

export const SEQ_PATTERNS = {
  run: 'Four in a row',
  trips: 'Three of a kind + one',
  pairs: 'Two pairs',
  akf: 'Ace, King, 4, 7',
};

// ranks: array of 4 ranks (1..13). Returns pattern key or null.
export function winPattern(ranks) {
  if (ranks.length !== 4) return null;
  const cnt = new Map();
  for (const r of ranks) cnt.set(r, (cnt.get(r) || 0) + 1);
  const counts = [...cnt.values()].sort((a, b) => b - a);
  if (counts[0] >= 3) return 'trips';
  if (counts[0] === 2 && counts[1] === 2) return 'pairs';
  if (cnt.size === 4) {
    const set = new Set(ranks);
    if (set.has(1) && set.has(13) && set.has(4) && set.has(7)) return 'akf';
    const ps = new Set(ranks.map(posOfRank));
    for (let start = 1; start <= 13; start++) {
      let all = true;
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) if (!ps.has(((start - 1 + k) % 13) + 1)) all = false;
      if (all) return 'run';
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function newSeqGame({ players, seed = (Math.random() * 2 ** 31) | 0 }) {
  if (players.length < MIN_PLAYERS || players.length > MAX_PLAYERS) throw new Error(`Special Sequence needs ${MIN_PLAYERS}-${MAX_PLAYERS} players`);
  const s = {
    seed,
    n: players.length,
    players: players.map((p, i) => ({ id: i, name: p.name, bot: p.bot || null, avatar: p.avatar || null, hand: [] })),
    dead: [],
    dispose: [],
    cur: 0,
    phase: 'pick', // pick → dispose
    turnNo: 1,
    over: null,
  };
  const deck = shuffle(Array.from({ length: 52 }, (_, i) => i), s);
  for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (const p of s.players) p.hand.push(deck.pop());
  s.dead = deck;
  return s;
}

function refill(s, ev) {
  if (s.dispose.length > 1) {
    const t = s.dispose.pop();
    s.dead = shuffle(s.dispose, s);
    s.dispose = [t];
    ev.push({ t: 'refill' });
  }
}

export function seqPick(s, from) {
  if (s.over || s.phase !== 'pick') return { ok: false, reason: 'Not now' };
  const ev = [];
  const p = s.players[s.cur];
  let card;
  if (from === 'dispose') {
    if (!s.dispose.length) return { ok: false, reason: 'The dispose pile is empty' };
    card = s.dispose.pop();
  } else {
    if (!s.dead.length) refill(s, ev);
    if (!s.dead.length) return { ok: false, reason: 'No cards left' };
    card = s.dead.pop();
  }
  p.hand.push(card);
  ev.push({ t: 'pick', p: s.cur, from, card });
  const pat = winPattern(p.hand.map(rankOf));
  if (pat) {
    s.over = { winner: s.cur, pattern: pat };
    ev.push({ t: 'win', p: s.cur, pattern: pat });
  } else s.phase = 'dispose';
  return { ok: true, ev };
}

export function seqDispose(s, card) {
  if (s.over || s.phase !== 'dispose') return { ok: false, reason: 'Not now' };
  const p = s.players[s.cur];
  const i = p.hand.indexOf(card);
  if (i < 0) return { ok: false, reason: 'Not in hand' };
  p.hand.splice(i, 1);
  s.dispose.push(card);
  const ev = [{ t: 'dispose', p: s.cur, card }];
  s.cur = (s.cur + 1) % s.n;
  s.turnNo++;
  s.phase = 'pick';
  ev.push({ t: 'turn', p: s.cur });
  return { ok: true, ev };
}

// ---- helpers for bots ----
// number of ranks that would complete a 3-card hand (weighted by copies still unseen)
export function outsWeight(three, unseen) {
  let w = 0;
  for (let r = 1; r <= 13; r++) if (winPattern([...three, r])) w += unseen[r];
  return w;
}

export function unseenCounts(known) {
  const u = new Array(14).fill(4);
  u[0] = 0;
  for (const id of known) u[rankOf(id)]--;
  return u.map((x) => Math.max(0, x));
}

=== www/js/sound.js ===
// All sound is synthesised with WebAudio, so the app needs no audio files and works offline.
let ctx = null, master = null;
let enabled = true, volume = 0.7, haptics = true;
let noiseBuf = null;

export function configure(o) {
  if ('enabled' in o) enabled = !!o.enabled;
  if ('volume' in o) { volume = o.volume; if (master) master.gain.value = volume; }
  if ('haptics' in o) haptics = !!o.haptics;
}

export function unlock() {
  try {
    if (!ctx) {
      const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AC) return;
      ctx = new AC();
      master = ctx.createGain();
      master.gain.value = volume;
      const comp = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
      master.connect(comp); comp.connect(ctx.destination);
      noiseBuf = ctx.createBuffer(1, ctx.sampleRate, ctx.sampleRate);
      const d = noiseBuf.getChannelData(0);
      for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    }
    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') ctx.resume();
  } catch (e) { /* audio unavailable */ }
}

function noise(dur, { f0 = 2500, f1 = null, q = 1, type = 'bandpass', gain = 0.4, when = 0, attack = 0.006 } = {}) {
  if (!ctx) return;
  const t = ctx.currentTime + when;
  const src = ctx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = noiseBuf; src.loop = true;
  const fl = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); fl.type = type; fl.Q.value = q;
  fl.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t);
  if (f1) fl.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f1, t + dur);
  const g = ctx.createGain();
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(gain, t + attack);
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
  src.connect(fl); fl.connect(g); g.connect(master);
  src.start(t, Math.random() * 0.5); src.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
}

function tone(freq, dur, { type = 'sine', gain = 0.25, when = 0, slideTo = null, attack = 0.006 } = {}) {
  if (!ctx) return;
  const t = ctx.currentTime + when;
  const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = type;
  o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
  if (slideTo) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(slideTo, t + dur);
  const g = ctx.createGain();
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(gain, t + attack);
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
  o.connect(g); g.connect(master);
  o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
}

const buzz = (p) => { if (haptics && navigator.vibrate) { try { navigator.vibrate(p); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } } };

const jit = (k = 0.12) => 1 - k + Math.random() * 2 * k; // a little variation so repeated cards never sound identical
// one paper "tick": the building block of a riffle or a slide
const tick1 = (when, gain = 0.14) => noise(0.022, { f0: (2600 + Math.random() * 3200), q: 1.6, gain: gain * jit(0.3), when, attack: 0.0012 });
const riffle = (n, t0, gap0, gap1, gain) => { let t = t0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { tick1(t, gain); t += gap0 + (gap1 - gap0) * (i / Math.max(1, n - 1)); } return t; };
// a card landing flat on the table: crisp snap, papery body, soft thump, tiny settle
const slap = (g = 1, when = 0) => {
  const j = jit();
  noise(0.04, { f0: 5200 * j, q: 0.7, type: 'highpass', gain: 0.3 * g, when, attack: 0.0015 });
  noise(0.1, { f0: 2400 * j, f1: 900, q: 1.1, gain: 0.34 * g, when, attack: 0.002 });
  tone(165 * j, 0.1, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.4 * g, slideTo: 68, attack: 0.002, when });
  noise(0.03, { f0: 3800, q: 1.2, gain: 0.13 * g, when: when + 0.024, attack: 0.001 });
};
const SFX = {
  click() { tone(520, 0.05, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.12 }); },
  select() { noise(0.035, { f0: 3200 * jit(), q: 1.4, gain: 0.16, attack: 0.001 }); tone(330, 0.05, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.07, attack: 0.002 }); },
  deal() { noise(0.1, { f0: 5200, f1: 1800, q: 0.8, gain: 0.26, attack: 0.004 }); slap(0.45, 0.085); },
  flip() { riffle(3, 0, 0.018, 0.012, 0.16); noise(0.05, { f0: 3600, f1: 1400, q: 1, gain: 0.2, when: 0.05, attack: 0.002 }); },
  place() { slap(1); buzz(8); },
  draw() {
    noise(0.2, { f0: 1500, f1: 4600, q: 0.6, gain: 0.2, attack: 0.04 });
    riffle(7, 0.01, 0.026, 0.014, 0.1);
    noise(0.05, { f0: 4200, q: 0.9, gain: 0.2, when: 0.19, attack: 0.002 });
  },
  shuffle() {
    let t = riffle(26, 0, 0.03, 0.011, 0.13);
    t = riffle(24, t + 0.07, 0.03, 0.011, 0.13);
    slap(0.7, t + 0.05); slap(0.5, t + 0.13);
  },
  turn() { tone(660, 0.09, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.14 }); tone(880, 0.12, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.14, when: 0.08 }); buzz(15); },
  tick() { tone(1200, 0.03, { type: 'square', gain: 0.05 }); },
  penalty() { tone(110, 0.35, { type: 'sawtooth', gain: 0.22, slideTo: 70 }); tone(116, 0.35, { type: 'sawtooth', gain: 0.18, slideTo: 74 }); buzz([30, 40, 30]); },
  skip() { tone(520, 0.08, { type: 'square', gain: 0.1 }); tone(330, 0.16, { type: 'square', gain: 0.1, when: 0.08 }); },
  reverse() { tone(420, 0.2, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.16, slideTo: 840 }); tone(840, 0.2, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.12, when: 0.2, slideTo: 420 }); },
  ace() { [523, 659, 784].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, 0.18, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.16, when: i * 0.06 })); },
  niko() { [392, 523, 659, 784].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, 0.28, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.2, when: i * 0.09 })); buzz([20, 30, 20]); },
  error() { tone(200, 0.12, { type: 'square', gain: 0.1 }); tone(160, 0.16, { type: 'square', gain: 0.1, when: 0.1 }); buzz(60); },
  star() { tone(300, 0.3, { type: 'sawtooth', gain: 0.14, slideTo: 120 }); buzz([60, 40, 60]); },
  out() { tone(220, 0.5, { type: 'sawtooth', gain: 0.16, slideTo: 60 }); },
  win() {
    [523, 659, 784, 1047, 784, 1047, 1319].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, 0.32, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.22, when: i * 0.11 }));
    tone(262, 1.0, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.18, when: 0.5 });
    buzz([40, 50, 40, 50, 120]);
  },
  lose() { [392, 349, 311, 262].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, 0.4, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.18, when: i * 0.2 })); },
};

export function sfx(name) {
  if (!enabled) return;
  unlock();
  const f = SFX[name];
  if (f && ctx) { try { f(); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } }
}

=== www/js/store.js ===
// Local profile, stats, history, inbox, chats and settings (localStorage, always wrapped in try/catch).
import { rpDelta, rankInfo } from './rp.js';

const KEY = 'nikokadi.v2';
export const START_RP = 10;

const DEFAULTS = () => ({
  name: 'You',
  avatar: 0, // face index, object id, or 'photo'
  photo: '', // uploaded picture (small data URL)
  rp: START_RP, // rank points, Niko Kadi only
  stats: {
    nk: { played: 0, won: 0, streak: 0, best: 0 },
    ss: { played: 0, won: 0, streak: 0, best: 0 },
  },
  vs: {}, // botId -> { w, l }
  history: [], // recent matches, newest first
  inbox: [], // notifications
  chats: [], // WhatsApp-style conversations
  seeded: false,
  chatsSeeded: false,
  streakDays: 0,
  lastDay: '',
  settings: { sound: true, volume: 0.7, haptics: true, reduceMotion: false, back: 'royal', felt: 'parlour', mode: 'light', theme: 'parlour', timer: true, chatBg: 'default' },
  rules: { flow: false, specialJK: false, liberal: false },
  game: 'nk',
  level: { nk: 2, ss: 2 }, // index into BOTS
  count: { nk: 2, ss: 2 }, // players at the table (2..4)
  lastBot: { nk: '', ss: '' },
  welcomed: false,
});

let data = null;
function merge(base, src) {
  for (const k of Object.keys(base)) {
    if (src && k in src) {
      if (base[k] && typeof base[k] === 'object' && !Array.isArray(base[k])) merge(base[k], src[k]);
      else base[k] = src[k];
    }
  }
  if (src && src.vs && typeof src.vs === 'object') base.vs = src.vs; // keep dynamic maps
  return base;
}

export function load() {
  if (data) return data;
  let raw = null;
  try { raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)); } catch (e) { raw = null; }
  data = merge(DEFAULTS(), raw);
  if (raw && !raw.themeV) { if (!raw.settings || raw.settings.felt === 'teal') data.settings.felt = 'parlour'; }
  data.themeV = 4;
  if (!['royal', 'guilloche', 'deco', 'beads', 'mandala', 'stars'].includes(data.settings.back)) data.settings.back = 'royal';
  for (const g of ['nk', 'ss']) data.count[g] = Math.max(2, Math.min(4, data.count[g] | 0 || 2)); // table cap: 4
  if (!data.seeded) { seedInbox(); data.seeded = true; }
  if (!data.chatsSeeded) { seedChats(); data.chatsSeeded = true; }
  return data;
}
export function save() {
  try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(data)); } catch (e) { /* storage unavailable */ }
}
export const get = () => load();

const dayStr = (t) => new Date(t).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

// Mark today as played (daily streak, shown as the flame).
export function touchDay() {
  const d = load();
  const t = dayStr(Date.now());
  if (d.lastDay !== t) {
    d.streakDays = d.lastDay === dayStr(Date.now() - 864e5) ? d.streakDays + 1 : 1;
    d.lastDay = t;
    save();
  }
}

function pingInbox() {
  try { document.dispatchEvent(new Event('nk:inbox')); } catch (e) { /* no DOM */ }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ inbox & notifications
let seq = 0;
export function addInbox(kind, { from = 'Niko Kadi', title, body }) {
  const d = load();
  d.inbox.unshift({ id: Date.now().toString(36) + (seq++).toString(36), kind, from, title, body, t: Date.now(), read: false });
  if (d.inbox.length > 60) d.inbox.length = 60;
  save();
  pingInbox();
}
export const inboxOf = (kind) => load().inbox.filter((m) => m.kind === kind);
export const unread = (kind) => {
  const d = load();
  const noteUnread = d.inbox.filter((m) => !m.read && m.kind === 'note').length;
  const chatUnread = d.chats.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (c.muted ? 0 : (c.unread || 0)), 0);
  if (kind === 'note') return noteUnread;
  if (kind === 'msg') return chatUnread;
  return noteUnread + chatUnread;
};
export function markRead(kind, id) {
  const d = load();
  for (const m of d.inbox) if (m.kind === kind && (!id || m.id === id)) m.read = true;
  save();
  pingInbox();
}
function seedInbox() {
  const d = data;
  const t = Date.now();
  d.inbox.push(
    { id: 'n1', kind: 'note', from: 'Niko Kadi', title: 'Your rank is Rookie', body: `You start with ${START_RP} RP. Win matches to move up.`, t: t - 500, read: false },
  );
}

export function getChats() {
  return load().chats.slice().sort((a, b) => {
    const ta = a.messages.length ? a.messages[a.messages.length - 1].t : 0;
    const tb = b.messages.length ? b.messages[b.messages.length - 1].t : 0;
    return tb - ta;
  });
}
export const getChat = (id) => load().chats.find((c) => c.id === id) || null;

export function addChat({ id, name, avatar, botId = null, online = true, status = 'Friend' }) {
  const d = load();
  if (id) {
    const hit = d.chats.find((c) => c.id === id);
    if (hit) return hit;
  }
  const c = {
    id: id || ('c_' + Date.now().toString(36) + (seq++).toString(36)),
    name,
    avatar,
    botId,
    online,
    muted: false,
    unread: 0,
    status,
    messages: [{ id: 's_' + Date.now().toString(36), from: 'system', text: `You are now connected with ${name}`, t: Date.now() }],
  };
  d.chats.unshift(c);
  save();
  pingInbox();
  return c;
}

export function sendMessage(chatId, msg) {
  const c = getChat(chatId);
  if (!c) return null;
  const full = { id: 'm_' + Date.now().toString(36) + (seq++).toString(36), t: Date.now(), reactions: [], ...msg };
  c.messages.push(full);
  if (c.messages.length > 200) c.messages.splice(0, c.messages.length - 200);
  if (full.from === 'them') c.unread = (c.unread || 0) + 1;
  save();
  pingInbox();
  return full;
}

export function markChatRead(chatId) {
  const c = getChat(chatId);
  if (!c || !c.unread) return;
  c.unread = 0;
  save();
  pingInbox();
}

export function updateMessage(chatId, msgId, patch) {
  const c = getChat(chatId);
  if (!c) return;
  const m = c.messages.find((x) => x.id === msgId);
  if (!m) return;
  Object.assign(m, patch);
  save();
}

export function deleteMessage(chatId, msgId) {
  updateMessage(chatId, msgId, { deleted: true, text: '', invite: null, reactions: [] });
}

export function toggleReaction(chatId, msgId, emoji) {
  const c = getChat(chatId);
  if (!c) return;
  const m = c.messages.find((x) => x.id === msgId);
  if (!m) return;
  m.reactions = m.reactions || [];
  const i = m.reactions.indexOf(emoji);
  if (i >= 0) m.reactions.splice(i, 1);
  else m.reactions.push(emoji);
  save();
}

export function clearChat(chatId) {
  const c = getChat(chatId);
  if (!c) return;
  c.messages = [];
  c.unread = 0;
  save();
  pingInbox();
}

export function deleteChat(chatId) {
  const d = load();
  d.chats = d.chats.filter((c) => c.id !== chatId);
  save();
  pingInbox();
}

export function toggleMuteChat(chatId) {
  const c = getChat(chatId);
  if (!c) return;
  c.muted = !c.muted;
  save();
  pingInbox();
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ results
// Record a finished game. Niko Kadi moves RP; Special Sequence earns no rank points.
export function recordResult(game, won, rivals, botId, info = {}) {
  const d = load();
  if (botId) d.lastBot[game] = botId;
  const s = d.stats[game];
  s.played++;
  if (won) { s.won++; s.streak++; s.best = Math.max(s.best, s.streak); } else s.streak = 0;
  let delta = 0, rankUp = null;
  if (game === 'nk') {
    const before = d.rp;
    delta = rpDelta(won, rivals.length + 1, rivals);
    d.rp = Math.max(0, before + delta);
    delta = d.rp - before;
    if (rankInfo(d.rp).idx > rankInfo(before).idx) rankUp = rankInfo(d.rp).name;
  }
  if (botId) {
    const k = game + ':' + botId;
    const v = d.vs[k] || (d.vs[k] = { w: 0, l: 0 });
    if (won) v.w++; else v.l++;
  }
  d.history.unshift({ g: game, t: Date.now(), won, delta, botId, count: rivals.length + 1, names: rivals.map((b) => b.name), winner: info.winner || '', rules: info.rules || null });
  if (d.history.length > 20) d.history.length = 20;
  touchDay();
  const who = rivals.map((b) => b.name).join(', ');
  const gname = game === 'nk' ? 'Niko Kadi' : 'Special Sequence';
  addInbox('note', {
    title: won ? `Victory in ${gname}` : `Defeat in ${gname}`,
    body: won ? `You beat ${who}.${game === 'nk' ? ` +${delta} RP.` : ''}` : `${info.winner || 'A rival'} won.${game === 'nk' && delta ? ` ${delta} RP.` : ''}`,
  });
  if (rankUp) addInbox('note', { title: `Promoted to ${rankUp}`, body: `You reached ${d.rp} RP. Keep climbing the leaderboard.` });
  save();
  return { delta, rp: d.rp, rankUp };
}
=== www/js/table.js ===
// Pieces shared by the Niko Kadi and Special Sequence tables: seats, plates, hand fan, flying cards, menu, results.
import * as store from './store.js';
import { avatarHTML } from './avatars.js';
import { backEl, cardEl } from './cards.js';
import { ICON } from './icons.js';
import { sfx, configure } from './sound.js';
import { $, $$, esc, sheet, closeSheet } from './ui.js';
import { HELP_NK, HELP_SS } from './help.js';

// where opponents sit, by table size, in play order after you
export const SLOTS = { 2: ['top'], 3: ['left', 'right'], 4: ['left', 'top', 'right'] };

export const reduced = () => !!store.get().settings.reduceMotion;
export const supportsFly = () => typeof CSS !== 'undefined' && CSS.supports && CSS.supports('translate', '1px');

// Fly a node from a rect to where it already is. Uses the individual translate/scale properties so the
// card's own transform (hand fan angle, pile tilt) is left alone.
export function fly(node, from, { dur = 360, delay = 0 } = {}) {
  if (!node || !from || reduced() || !supportsFly() || !node.animate) return;
  const b = node.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (!b.width) return;
  const dx = from.left + from.width / 2 - (b.left + b.width / 2);
  const dy = from.top + from.height / 2 - (b.top + b.height / 2);
  const sc = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(2.5, from.width / b.width));
  node.animate(
    [{ translate: `${dx}px ${dy}px`, scale: sc }, { translate: '0px 0px', scale: 1 }],
    { duration: dur, delay, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.2,.75,.25,1)', fill: 'backwards' }
  );
}

// a card back that flies between two rects and disappears (e.g. a draw going to an opponent)
export function flyBack(from, to, { dur = 340, delay = 0 } = {}) {
  if (!from || !to || reduced() || !supportsFly()) return;
  const el = backEl();
  el.style.cssText = `position:fixed;left:${to.left + to.width / 2 - 14}px;top:${to.top + to.height / 2 - 20}px;width:28px;height:39px;--cw:28px;z-index:70;pointer-events:none`;
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  const dx = from.left + from.width / 2 - (to.left + to.width / 2);
  const dy = from.top + from.height / 2 - (to.top + to.height / 2);
  const a = el.animate(
    [{ translate: `${dx}px ${dy}px`, scale: 1.7, opacity: 0.95 }, { translate: '0 0', scale: 1, opacity: 0.2 }],
    { duration: dur, delay, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.3,.7,.3,1)', fill: 'both' }
  );
  a.finished.catch(() => {}).then(() => el.remove());
}

export const rl = (r) => ({ 1: 'A', 11: 'J', 12: 'Q', 13: 'K' }[r] || String(r));
export const suitHTML = (s) => `<span class="${s === 1 || s === 2 ? 'red' : ''}">${['♠', '♥', '♦', '♣'][s]}︎</span>`;

export function plateHTML(p, i, { active = false, tag = '' } = {}) {
  const stars = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, j) => `<i class="${j < (p.stars ?? 5) ? '' : 'off'}"></i>`).join('');
  const t = p.out ? '<span class="tag skip">OUT</span>' : tag;
  return `<div class="plate${active ? ' active' : ''}${p.out ? ' out' : ''}" data-p="${i}">
    ${avatarHTML(p.avatar, 34)}
    <div class="nm"><b>${esc(p.name)}</b>${p.stars != null ? `<span class="stars">${stars}</span>` : ''}</div>
    <span class="cnt">${p.hand.length}</span>${t}
    <div class="tbar"><i></i></div></div>`;
}

export function fanHTML(n) {
  return `<div class="fan">${Array.from({ length: Math.min(n, 8) }, () => backEl().outerHTML).join('')}</div>`;
}

// lay my cards out in a fan; els are .card nodes already inside the hand container
export function layoutHand(container, els) {
  const n = els.length;
  const W = container.clientWidth || 360;
  // big hands get smaller cards and a flatter fan, so nothing slides off the sides or the bottom
  const base = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(container.parentElement || container).getPropertyValue('--hcw')) || 80;
  const f = n <= 5 ? 1 : n <= 7 ? 0.92 : n <= 9 ? 0.82 : 0.72;
  container.style.setProperty('--hcw', Math.round(base * f) + 'px');
  const cw = Math.round(base * f);
  const room = W - cw - 24;
  const step = n > 1 ? Math.min(cw * 0.7, room / (n - 1)) : 0;
  const mid = (n - 1) / 2;
  els.forEach((el, i) => {
    const k = i - mid;
    el.style.setProperty('--x', k * step + 'px');
    el.style.setProperty('--y', (Math.min(10, k * k * (n > 5 ? 0.6 : 2.2)) - (n > 5 ? 14 : 0)) + 'px');
    el.style.setProperty('--r', (n > 1 ? k * Math.min(4.5, 16 / n) : 0) + 'deg');
    el.style.zIndex = i + 1;
  });
}

// Pause / menu sheet. Calls back so each table can pause its own timers.
export function menuSheet({ onResume, onResign, helpHTML, resignText = 'Resign' }) {
  const d = store.get();
  const sh = sheet(
    `<h2>Paused</h2>
    <button class="opt" id="mSound"><span class="ic">${d.settings.sound ? ICON.sound : ICON.mute}</span><div>Sound ${d.settings.sound ? 'on' : 'off'}<small>Tap to switch</small></div></button>
    <button class="opt" id="mHelp"><span class="ic">${ICON.info}</span><div>How to play<small>Rules and special cards</small></div></button>
    <button class="opt" id="mResign"><span class="ic" style="color:var(--danger)">${ICON.close}</span><div>${resignText}</div></button>
    <div class="row"><button class="btn block" id="mGo">Resume</button></div>`,
    { center: true, onClose: onResume }
  );
  $('#mGo', sh.el).onclick = () => { sfx('click'); closeSheet(); };
  $('#mSound', sh.el).onclick = () => {
    d.settings.sound = !d.settings.sound;
    store.save();
    configure({ enabled: d.settings.sound });
    menuSheetRefresh(sh.el, d);
    if (d.settings.sound) sfx('click');
  };
  $('#mHelp', sh.el).onclick = () => {
    sfx('click');
    const h = sheet(`<h2>How to play</h2>${helpHTML}<div class="row"><button class="btn grey block" id="ok">Back</button></div>`, { onClose: () => menuSheet({ onResume, onResign, helpHTML, resignText }) });
    $('#ok', h.el).onclick = () => closeSheet();
  };
  $('#mResign', sh.el).onclick = () => {
    sfx('click');
    const c = sheet(`<h2>Leave the game?</h2><div class="row"><button class="btn grey" id="n">Keep playing</button><button class="btn danger" id="y">Leave</button></div>`, { center: true, onClose: onResume });
    $('#n', c.el).onclick = () => closeSheet();
    $('#y', c.el).onclick = () => { closeSheet(true); onResign(); };
  };
}
function menuSheetRefresh(el, d) {
  const b = $('#mSound', el);
  b.innerHTML = `<span class="ic">${d.settings.sound ? ICON.sound : ICON.mute}</span><div>Sound ${d.settings.sound ? 'on' : 'off'}<small>Tap to switch</small></div>`;
}

export { HELP_NK, HELP_SS, cardEl };

// Pointer-based dragging for a hand card or a pile. A tap (no real movement) calls onTap.
//   can()        may a drag start right now?
//   ghost()      node that follows the finger (a clone of the card, or a card back)
//   rectOf()     where the dragged thing starts (defaults to the element itself)
//   hit(x, y)    is the pointer over the drop zone?
//   hi()         element to highlight while over the zone
//   onDrop()     runs on a good drop; a bad drop glides the card back home
export function dragify(el, { can, ghost, rectOf, hit, hi, onDrop, onTap, target }) {
  let st = null;
  const THR = 9;
  const clear = () => {
    window.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
    window.removeEventListener('pointerup', up);
    window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', cancel);
  };
  function begin(e) {
    const r = rectOf ? rectOf() : el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!r) { st.can = false; return; }
    const g = ghost();
    g.classList.add('drag-ghost');
    g.style.width = r.width + 'px';
    g.style.height = r.height + 'px';
    g.style.left = r.left + 'px';
    g.style.top = r.top + 'px';
    g.style.setProperty('--cw', r.width + 'px');
    document.body.appendChild(g);
    st.drag = true; st.g = g; st.r = r; st.ox = st.x - r.left; st.oy = st.y - r.top;
    if (el.classList.contains('card')) el.classList.add('dragging');
  }
  // dragged at least halfway to the target counts as a drop, so the move completes itself
  const reach = (e) => {
    if (hit(e.clientX, e.clientY)) return true;
    const T = target && target();
    if (!T || !st) return false;
    const gx = e.clientX - st.ox + st.r.width / 2, gy = e.clientY - st.oy + st.r.height / 2;
    const sx = st.r.left + st.r.width / 2, sy = st.r.top + st.r.height / 2;
    const tx = T.left + T.width / 2, ty = T.top + T.height / 2;
    const D = Math.hypot(tx - sx, ty - sy);
    return D > 0 && Math.hypot(tx - gx, ty - gy) <= D * 0.5;
  };
  function move(e) {
    if (!st || e.pointerId !== st.id) return;
    if (!st.drag) {
      if (!st.can || Math.hypot(e.clientX - st.x, e.clientY - st.y) < THR) return;
      begin(e);
      if (!st.drag) return;
    }
    st.g.style.left = e.clientX - st.ox + 'px';
    st.g.style.top = e.clientY - st.oy + 'px';
    const over = reach(e);
    if (over !== st.over) { st.over = over; const h = hi && hi(); if (h) h.classList.toggle('drop-hi', over); }
    if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
  }
  function finish(e, allowDrop) {
    if (!st || (e && e.pointerId !== st.id)) return;
    const s = st;
    st = null;
    clear();
    if (!s.drag) { if (allowDrop && onTap) onTap(e); return; }
    const h = hi && hi();
    if (h) h.classList.remove('drop-hi');
    el.classList.remove('dragging');
    st = s;
    const ok = allowDrop && reach(e);
    st = null;
    if (ok) { s.g.remove(); onDrop(); return; }
    s.g.classList.add('home');
    s.g.style.left = s.r.left + 'px';
    s.g.style.top = s.r.top + 'px';
    setTimeout(() => s.g.remove(), 220);
  }
  const up = (e) => finish(e, true);
  const cancel = (e) => finish(e, false);
  el.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
    if (st || (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0)) return;
    st = { id: e.pointerId, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, drag: false, can: !!can(), over: false };
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', move, { passive: false });
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', up);
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', cancel);
  });
}

=== www/js/ui.js ===
// Small UI helpers shared by all screens: DOM, toast, bottom sheets.
export const $ = (sel, root = document) => root.querySelector(sel);
export const $$ = (sel, root = document) => [...root.querySelectorAll(sel)];

export function h(html) {
  const t = document.createElement('template');
  t.innerHTML = html.trim();
  return t.content.firstElementChild;
}

export const esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]));

let toastTimer = 0;
export function toast(msg, ms = 1700) {
  const t = $('#toast');
  if (!t) return;
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.hidden = false;
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => (t.hidden = true), ms);
}

let closeCb = null;
// Show a bottom sheet (or centred dialog). Returns { el, close }.
export function sheet(html, { center = false, dismiss = true, onClose = null } = {}) {
  const o = $('#overlay');
  closeSheet(true);
  o.className = center ? 'ovc' : '';
  o.innerHTML = `<div class="sheet">${html}</div>`;
  o.hidden = false;
  closeCb = onClose;
  o.onclick = (e) => {
    if (dismiss && e.target === o) closeSheet();
  };
  return { el: o.firstElementChild, close: closeSheet };
}

export function closeSheet(silent = false) {
  const o = $('#overlay');
  if (!o || o.hidden) return;
  o.hidden = true;
  o.innerHTML = '';
  const cb = closeCb;
  closeCb = null;
  if (!silent && cb) cb();
}
export const sheetOpen = () => { const o = $('#overlay'); return !!o && !o.hidden; };

=== tests/engine.test.mjs ===
import * as E from '../www/js/engine.js';
const { cardId: C } = E;
let pass = 0, fail = 0;
const t = (name, fn) => {
  try { fn(); pass++; } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('FAIL', name, '\n  ', e.message); }
};
const eq = (a, b, m = '') => { if (JSON.stringify(a) !== JSON.stringify(b)) throw new Error(`${m} expected ${JSON.stringify(b)} got ${JSON.stringify(a)}`); };
const ok = (r, m = '') => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`${m} expected ok, got: ${r.reason}`); };
const no = (r, m = '') => { if (r.ok) throw new Error(`${m} expected rejection`); };

// build a state with exact hands
function mk({ hands, top = '5H', rules = {}, cur = 0, extra = [] }) {
  const s = E.newGame({ players: hands.map((_, i) => ({ name: 'P' + (i + 1) })), rules, seed: 7 });
  const used = new Set();
  s.players.forEach((p, i) => { p.hand = hands[i].map(C); p.hand.forEach((c) => used.add(c)); });
  const topId = C(top); used.add(topId);
  s.discard = [topId];
  s.draw = E.shuffle(Array.from({ length: 54 }, (_, i) => i).filter((c) => !used.has(c)), s);
  s.cur = cur;
  return s;
}
const play = (s, str, opts) => E.place(s, str.split(' ').map(C), opts);

t('match by suit / rank, illegal otherwise', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['9H', '4C', '10S'], ['5S']], top: '5H' });
  no(play(s, '4C'));
  ok(play(s, '9H'));
});

t('multiple same-rank in one move, then pass', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['5H', '5S', '9S', 'KD'], ['6C', '7C', '4C']], top: '5D' });
  ok(play(s, '5H 5S'));
  ok(play(s, '9S'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
  eq(s.cur, 1);
});

t('Queen and 8 are the same rank', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['QH', '8S', '6S', '4C'], ['4D', '5D']], top: '9H' });
  ok(play(s, 'QH 8S'));
  eq(E.ctx(s), 'answer');
  no(play(s, '4C'), 'wrong suit answer'); // 8S on top -> needs spade
  ok(play(s, '6S'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
});

t('unanswered question => draw one, turn ends', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['QH', '8S', 'KC'], ['4D', '5D']], top: '9H' });
  ok(play(s, 'QH 8S'));
  const before = s.players[0].hand.length;
  ok(E.take(s));
  eq(s.players[0].hand.length, before + 1);
  eq(s.cur, 1);
});

t('penalty: 2D then red joker => next draws 7 (spec example)', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['2D', '4C'], ['RJ', '4S'], ['5C'], ['9S']], top: '5D' });
  ok(play(s, '2D'));
  eq(s.cur, 1); eq(s.pending.count, 2);
  ok(play(s, 'RJ'));
  eq(s.cur, 2); eq(s.pending.count, 7);
  const h = s.players[2].hand.length;
  ok(E.take(s));
  eq(s.players[2].hand.length, h + 7);
});

t('penalty strict: black joker allowed on red joker, spade 2 not on red joker', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['RJ', '4C'], ['2S', '9S'], ['BJ'], ['5C']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, 'RJ'));
  no(play(s, '2S'));
  ok(E.take(s)); // 5 cards... P2 has 2 cards +5 = 7 ok
  // after RJ + draw, next player matches red (top red joker)
});

t('penalty stack: red joker + 2H + 2S in one move', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['RJ', '2H', '2S', '9C'], ['4D']], top: '5H' });
  const r = E.findOrder(s, ['RJ', '2H', '2S'].map(C));
  eq(r && r.length, 3);
  ok(E.place(s, r));
  eq(s.pending.count, 9);
});

t('penalty strict: 2H + 3S not stackable in one move; liberal allows', () => {
  let s = mk({ hands: [['2H', '3S', '9C'], ['4D']], top: '5H' });
  no(play(s, '2H 3S'));
  s = mk({ hands: [['2H', '3S', '9C'], ['4D']], top: '5H', rules: { liberal: true } });
  ok(play(s, '2H 3S'));
  eq(s.pending.count, 5);
});

t('ace cancels penalty and returns to prior suit', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['3H', '9C'], ['AC', '4D'], ['4S']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, '3H'));
  ok(play(s, 'AC'));
  eq(s.pending, null);
  eq(s.cur, 2);
  eq(E.info(s).mask, 2); // hearts
  no(play(s, '4S'));
});

t('ace flow variant: forward penalty', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['3H', '9C'], ['AC', '4D'], ['4S']], top: '5H', rules: { flow: true } });
  ok(play(s, '3H'));
  ok(play(s, 'AC', { mode: 'forward' }));
  eq(s.pending.count, 3); eq(s.cur, 2);
});

t('ace: suit demand binds next player', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['AD', '9C'], ['4S', '6H'], ['5C']], top: '5H' });
  no(play(s, 'AD'), 'needs suit');
  ok(play(s, 'AD', { demand: { suit: 0 } }));
  eq(s.demand.W, 1);
  no(play(s, '6H'));
  ok(play(s, '4S'));
  eq(s.demand, null);
});

t('two aces request a specific card; players draw until played; ace cancels', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['AD', 'AC', '9C'], ['4S', '6H'], ['5C', 'AH']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, 'AD AC', { demand: { suit: 0, rank: 4 } }));
  eq(s.demand.W, 2);
  ok(play(s, '4S')); // P2 has the card
  eq(s.demand, null);
  // second run: P2 lacks it
  const s2 = mk({ hands: [['AD', 'AC', '9C'], ['6H', '7H'], ['AH', '5C']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s2, 'AD AC', { demand: { suit: 0, rank: 4 } }));
  no(play(s2, '6H'));
  ok(E.take(s2)); // P2 draws one
  eq(s2.cur, 2);
  ok(play(s2, 'AH')); // one normal ace cancels one demand -> suit demand remains
  eq(s2.demand.W, 1); eq(s2.demand.suit, 0);
});

t('ace congress (A♠) = two aces => request', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['AS', '9C'], ['4D']], top: '5H' });
  const a = E.analyze(s, [C('AS')]);
  eq(a.needs, 'card');
});

t('lock: 3 aces worth => 2 aces reduce, 3 cancel', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['AD', 'AC', 'AH', '9C'], ['AS', '6H'], ['AD']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, 'AD AC AH', { demand: { suit: 0, rank: 4 } }));
  eq(s.demand.W, 3);
  // P2 holds A♠ (worth 2) -> reduces to suit demand, no leftover
  ok(play(s, 'AS'));
  eq(s.demand.W, 1);
});

t('lock: one ace cannot touch a locked demand', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['AD', 'AC', 'AH', '9C'], ['AD'.replace('D', 'S') === 'AS' ? '5S' : '5S', '6H']], top: '5H' });
  s.players[1].hand.push(C('AC') === C('AC') ? 13 * 2 : 0); // A♦ is id 26
  ok(play(s, 'AD AC AH', { demand: { suit: 0, rank: 4 } }));
  const a = E.analyze(s, [26]);
  eq(a.ok, false);
});

t('ace congress cancelling a suit demand leaves leftover single demand', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['AD', '9C'], ['AS', '6H'], ['4S']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, 'AD', { demand: { suit: 2 } }));
  const a = E.analyze(s, [C('AS')]);
  eq(a.needs, 'suit');
  ok(play(s, 'AS', { demand: { suit: 3 } }));
  eq(s.demand.suit, 3);
});

t('jack skips next; counter with jack (spec example)', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['JH', 'JS', '9C'], ['JD', 'JC', '4C'], ['5C'], ['6C']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, 'JH JS'));
  ok(E.pass(s)); // skips P2? P2 has jacks -> prompted
  eq(s.cur, 1); eq(s.threat, 2);
  ok(play(s, 'JD JC')); // counter + add another
  eq(s.carry, 1); eq(s.turn.jumps, 1);
  ok(E.pass(s));
  // P3 and P4 skipped, back to P1
  eq(s.cur, 0);
});

t('jack without counter just skips', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['JH', '9C'], ['4S'], ['6C']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, 'JH'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
  eq(s.cur, 2);
});

t('two players: jack gives another turn', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['JH', '9C', '4H'], ['4S']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, 'JH'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
  eq(s.cur, 0);
});

t('king reverses (1-2-3-4, P2 plays king => P1 next)', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['4C'], ['KH', '9C'], ['5S'], ['6S']], top: '5H', cur: 1 });
  ok(play(s, 'KH'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
  eq(s.cur, 0);
});

t('two kings = no effect', () => {

  ok(play(s, 'KH KS'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
  eq(s.cur, 2);
});

t('two players: king does not alter order', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['KH', '9C'], ['4S']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, 'KH'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
  eq(s.cur, 1);
});

t('special J&K in pending: jack forwards, king bounces back and reverses', () => {

  ok(play(s, '3H'));
  ok(play(s, 'JH'));
  eq(s.pending.count, 3); eq(s.cur, 2);
  s = mk({ hands: [['9C'], ['3H', '4S'], ['KH', '4D'], ['6S']], top: '5H', rules: { specialJK: true }, cur: 1 });
  ok(play(s, '3H'));
  eq(s.cur, 2);
  ok(play(s, 'KH'));
  eq(s.cur, 1); eq(s.dir, -1); eq(s.pending.count, 3);
});

t('default: jack cannot be used against a penalty', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['3H', '9C'], ['JH', '4D'], ['5S']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, '3H'));
  no(play(s, 'JH'));
});

t('win: declared earlier, last move wins', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['5S', '9S'], ['4S', '6D', '7D']], top: '5H' });
  ok(E.declare(s)); // 5S then 9S chain => ready
  ok(play(s, '5S'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
  eq(s.players[0].declared !== null, true);
  ok(play(s, '4S'));
  ok(E.pass(s));
  const r = play(s, '9S');
  ok(r); eq(s.over.winner, 0);
});

t('cannot win without declaring', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['5S'], ['4S', '6D', '7D']], top: '5H' });
  no(play(s, '5S'));
});

t('declaration is dropped if hand no longer ready; drawing resets it', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['5S', '9S', 'AC'], ['4S']], top: '5H' });
  eq(E.canFall(s.players[0].hand), false); // ace in hand
  no(E.declare(s));
});

t('cannot win same turn as declaring', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['5S', '9S'], ['4D']], top: '5H' });
  ok(E.declare(s));
  no(play(s, '5S 9S')); // different rank anyway
  const s2 = mk({ hands: [['5S', '5C'], ['4D']], top: '5H' });
  ok(E.declare(s2));
  no(play(s2, '5S 5C'), 'same turn');
});

t('question can be part of a winning move (Q then answer)', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['QH', '6H'], ['4D', '5C', '6C']], top: '5H' });
  eq(E.canFall(s.players[0].hand), true);
  ok(E.declare(s));
  ok(play(s, 'QH' ? '6H' : ''));
  // note: after 6H, hand is QH only -> not ready; declaration dropped at endTurn
});

t('niko path: Q then answer wins', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['QH', '6H'], ['4D', '5C', '6C']], top: '5H' });
  s.players[0].declared = 0; // declared on a previous turn
  const path = E.winPath(s);
  eq(path && path.length, 2);
  ok(E.place(s, path[0])); ok(E.place(s, path[1]));
  eq(s.over.winner, 0);
});

t('eight-card limit disqualifies', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['3H', '9C'], ['4D', '5D', '6D', '7D', '9D', '10D', '4H', '5H']], top: '5H' });
  ok(play(s, '3H'));
  ok(E.take(s));
  eq(s.players[1].out, true);
  eq(s.over.winner, 0);
});

t('timeout costs a star and draws', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['3H', '9C'], ['4D']], top: '5H' });
  const r = E.timeout(s);
  ok(r);
  eq(s.players[0].stars, 4);
  eq(s.cur, 1);
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { s.cur = 0; E.timeout(s); if (s.over) break; }
  eq(s.players[0].out, true);
});

t('refill reshuffles discard', () => {
  const s = mk({ hands: [['3H', '9C'], ['4D']], top: '5H' });
  s.discard = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, C('5H')];
  s.draw = [];
  ok(E.take(s));
  eq(s.players[0].hand.length, 3);
  eq(s.discard.length, 1);
});

console.log(`${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== tests/sim.test.mjs ===
import * as E from '../www/js/engine.js';
import { BOTS, chooseAction, applyAction, seqChoosePick, seqChooseDispose } from '../www/js/bots.js';
import { newSeqGame, seqPick, seqDispose } from '../www/js/sequence.js';

function rngFrom(seed){ let a=seed|0; return ()=>{ a=(a+0x6d2b79f5)|0; let t=Math.imul(a^(a>>>15),1|a); t^=t+Math.imul(t^(t>>>7),61|t); return ((t^(t>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296; }; }

function playNiko(bots, rules, seed){
  const rng=rngFrom(seed);
  const s=E.newGame({players:bots.map(b=>({name:b.name,bot:b})),rules,seed});
  let steps=0, stats={take:0, place:0};
  while(!s.over){
    if(++steps>4000) return {stuck:true, s};
    const L=s.players[s.cur].bot.L;
    const a=chooseAction(s,L,rng);
    const r=applyAction(s,a);
    if(!r.ok){ return {error:`bad action ${JSON.stringify(a)} -> ${r.reason}`, s}; }
    stats[a.type]=(stats[a.type]||0)+1;
    // invariants
    const all=[...s.draw,...s.discard,...s.players.flatMap(p=>p.hand)];
    if(new Set(all).size!==all.length) return {error:'duplicate cards', s};
    if(all.length!==54) return {error:'card count '+all.length, s};
  }
  return {winner:s.over.winner, reason:s.over.reason, steps, stats};
}

const N=+process.env.N||200;
let stuck=0, errors=0, wins={}, reasons={}, totalSteps=0, played=0;
const rulesets=[{}, {flow:true}, {specialJK:true}, {liberal:true}, {flow:true,specialJK:true,liberal:true}];
for(let g=0; g<N; g++){
  const n=2+(g%3);
  const bots=[]; for(let i=0;i<n;i++) bots.push(BOTS[(g*3+i*5)%BOTS.length]);
  const r=playNiko(bots, rulesets[g%rulesets.length], 1000+g);
  if(r.stuck){stuck++; if(stuck<3) console.log('STUCK', g, E.ctx(r.s), r.s.players.map(p=>p.hand.map(E.cardName).join(' ')), E.cardName(E.top(r.s)), r.s.pending, r.s.demand, r.s.threat);}
  else if(r.error){errors++; console.log('ERR',g,r.error);}
  else {played++; totalSteps+=r.steps; reasons[r.reason]=(reasons[r.reason]||0)+1;}
}
console.log(`niko: ${played} finished, ${stuck} stuck, ${errors} errors, avg steps ${(totalSteps/Math.max(1,played)).toFixed(0)}`, reasons);

// strength ladder: 2-player games, tiers head to head
function duel(a,b,games){ let aw=0,done=0; for(let g=0;g<games;g++){ const order=g%2? [b,a]:[a,b]; const r=playNiko(order,{}, 5000+g); if(r.winner===undefined) continue; done++; if(order[r.winner]===a) aw++; } return `${a.name} beat ${b.name}: ${aw}/${done}`; }
const M=+process.env.M||60;
console.log(duel(BOTS[5],BOTS[0],M)); console.log(duel(BOTS[4],BOTS[1],M)); console.log(duel(BOTS[3],BOTS[1],M)); console.log(duel(BOTS[5],BOTS[3],M));

// sequence
let sd=0, sst=0, sw={};
for(let g=0; g<300; g++){
  const rng=rngFrom(g);
  const n=2+(g%3);
  const s=newSeqGame({players:Array.from({length:n},(_,i)=>({name:'b'+i,bot:BOTS[(g+i*3)%BOTS.length]})),seed:g});
  let steps=0;
  while(!s.over && steps<2000){ steps++; const L=s.players[s.cur].bot.L;
    if(s.phase==='pick'){ const r=seqPick(s, seqChoosePick(s,L,rng)); if(!r.ok) throw new Error(r.reason); }
    else { const r=seqDispose(s, seqChooseDispose(s,L,rng)); if(!r.ok) throw new Error(r.reason); } }
  if(s.over){ sd++; sst+=steps; sw[s.over.pattern]=(sw[s.over.pattern]||0)+1; }
}
console.log(`sequence: ${sd}/300 finished, avg steps ${(sst/Math.max(1,sd)).toFixed(0)}`, sw);
